Getter gains `width` (meter precedent). Sampler-verified (9422): `#fixed {…,width:"320px"}` + computed `width:320px`, `#plain` unset + `min-width:160px`, registry **114→115**, 0 orphans. Next-active → dd track opens.

> **M-RP5.0 `entity-avatar` ✅ DONE (J-462):** the **dd track opens for real** — the **first data-dependent component** (dd-**atomic**), the first `data-dependent/` occupant. Materializes ONE address-book entry (identity or space) into a visual; **shape branches on the data** (dd ≠ di): identity + DM space = circle, non-DM space = rounded-square. **The dd-root rule (N-075):** dd does NOT inherit the di `<div>`=composite litmus — root = honest HTML for the materialized thing (`<figure role="img">`, `aria-label={name ?? kind}`, `<figcaption>` reserved for `labeled`/`card`). Source-agnostic **`EntityDescriptor { kind, name?, id, flags{isAi?,revoked?,isDm?,e2e?}, image? }`** seam = the **W-11 dd-socket** payload; `core` imports **no** `IdentityRecord`/`SpaceState` (shell owns the protocol → descriptor map). Colour = shared **`seedColour(name ?? id)`** base helper **factored out of `chip`** (byte-identical, chip re-verified 0-regression; no `--accent` dependency → shell-independent). `initials` = 1–2 graphemes of `name` (grapheme-safe), absent → xgid-tail fallback. Primary axis = **`variant`** (purpose): `presence` (xs, glyph) / `list` (sm, + initials) — size/content derived. Self-drawn badges (not a nested `led`): `isAi` `::after` violet spark, `revoked` `grayscale`+`::before` slash. `onActivate?` reserved (menu seam, M-RP5.3). Getter `{ kind, variant, name, initials, seed, flags }`. Sampler DD·atomic panel populated, CDP-verified (9422): registry **115→124** (9 cells), FIGURE/role=img, shape-per-kind, `absent→"AZ"` fallback, seed client↔node identical (`seedMatch:true`), 8 `.entity-avatar*` rules in cascade, chip 0-regression, 0 orphans. Next-active → `container-list-item` (dd-composite, composes entity-avatar; unlocks `labeled`/`card`) → `spaces-panel` → `temperature-indicator` widget. Track A (J-461) gates the status-bearing avatar variants (M-RP5.2).

> **M-RP5.1 `entity-item` ✅ DONE (J-463):** the **first dd-composite** (renamed from `container-list-item`), the second `data-dependent/` occupant — a variant-driven display unit that materializes an address-book entry (identity ∪ space ∪ DM) as avatar + name + optional secondary + trailing meta/status. Root `<div class="entity-item">` (N-075). **Single-knob derive:** the consumer sets one `entity-item` `variant` (`row`/`card`/`nav`/`inline`); the composite derives the inner `entity-avatar` variant (`row→list · card→card · nav→labeled · inline→presence`). Slot surface derived per variant (row = name+meta · card = name+secondary+status · nav/inline = name); secondary/status/meta = **caller-supplied slots** (source-agnostic; shell maps protocol + Track A `state.status`). Composes the **real** `entity-avatar` child (self-registers `__avatar` → the matrix multiplies, status-indicator precedent). **Global width rule (N-076):** unset → 100%, set → value, `min-width` = slot-composition floor (retro-references `meter`/`section`, which already ship the shape). **`entity-avatar` amendment (additive, pre-announced at M-RP5.0):** `variant` widened `+ labeled | card` (render initials at ascending sizes list 28 / labeled 32 / card 40; 0-regression on presence/list). Sampler DD·composite panel populated, CDP-verified (9422): registry **124→138** (+14 = 7 composites + 7 `__avatar` children), 0 orphans, derive-map literal, width unset→180 floor / set→280px, root `DIV.entity-item`, 14 `.entity-item*` rules in cascade, card density border + `--s2`. Next-active → `spaces-panel` (composes `section` + `entity-item ×N`, owns roving focus) → `entity-context-menu` widget (consumes `onActivate?`) → `temperature-indicator` widget (W-11 dd-socket). Track A gates the status-bearing variants (M-RP5.2).

> **M-RP5.1a `status` + M-RP5.1b avatar corner-slot ✅ DONE (J-464):** the self-status **dd-atomic** (the 2nd, after `entity-avatar`) + the additive `entity-avatar` `status?` corner-slot. Materializes Track A `state.status` (J-461) — personal **expression** (emoji + short line), NOT presence — via a source-agnostic view-model `{emoji?,text?,updatedAt?,expiresAt?}` (`core` imports no protocol type). **Variants** `badge` (emoji corner) / `line` (emoji + text) / `full` (+ relative "updated Nm ago"). Root `<span class="status" role="img">` (badge) / `<span class="status">` (line/full); getter `{variant,emoji,hasText,expired}`; `title` tooltip fallback. **Mounted-but-empty on expiry (Joe-locked, corrects the runbook's "not rendered"):** the root `<span use:envelope>` always mounts (getter always registers, `expired:true` CDP-readable), rendering no content when expired/content-less — `data-empty` collapses it. **Avatar seam (5.1b):** `entity-avatar` gains `status?`, rendering `<Status variant="badge" id=__status>` as a bottom-right corner overlay (child self-registers; avatar getter unchanged → 0-regression). **Collision fix (D-065):** combobox/tag-select popups already use a bare ad-hoc `.status`; the base rule is scoped `.status[data-variant]` (the component always emits it; the popups never do) → no leak into those closed components. Sampler DD·atomic cells + avatar-with-status, CDP-verified (9422): registry **138→146** (+8 = 6 standalone + `entity-avatar#with-status` + its `__status`), 0 orphans; expired registers `{expired:true}` on a collapsed span; `full` = "🎧 in a meeting updated 5m ago"; corner child `position:absolute -3px` inside the avatar; avatar/item cells unchanged; bare-`.status` probe `display:inline` (leak gone). Next-active → `spaces-panel` (M-RP5.2, inherits status via the avatar) → `entity-context-menu` widget (uses `status full`).

> **M-RP5.2 `entity-panel` ✅ DONE (J-465):** the **last dd-composite** — closes the dd-composite tier (widget tier next). A roving-focus, single-select **listbox** that materializes a list of entries: a `section` (group chrome, self-registers `__section`) wrapping a `<ul role="listbox">` of `entity-item` option rows; the panel owns the keyboard focus + selection + empty state (which `entity-item` deferred to it). `spaces-panel` is a consumer **preset** (title + spaces items), not a separate component. **Rooting ("wrap, not beside"):** the outermost DOM is the `<section>`; the panel's own getter rides the `<ul class="entity-panel" use:envelope>` inside — the panel IS the listbox. **Focus:** roving tabindex (one row `0`, rest `-1`), Arrows/Home/End move + focus, Enter/Space + click select+activate; `selected` ($bindable id) → `aria-selected` + the row's `[data-selected]`. Empty → a composed `paragraph` message + `hasEmpty:true`; collapse pass-through to `section` hides rows (slot mounted). Getter `{count,selected,collapsed,hasEmpty}`. **+Corner-fix H (skin-only):** `entity-avatar` isAi spark → **top-right** (was bottom-right), de-colliding with the bottom-right status badge. **+`entity-item` status-forward amendment (D-065):** a variant shows status **once** — `card` = inline line (no avatar badge), `row`/`nav`/`inline` = avatar corner badge (`avatarStatus = variant==='card' ? undefined : status`); the wiring Phase-0's "for free" assumed but M-RP5.1 never added; `card-space` 0-regression verified. Sampler DD·composite cells, CDP-verified (9422): getters exact (bind:selected seeded); registry **146→173** (+27), 0 orphans; `role=listbox`/`option`; roving tabindex `["-1","0","-1"]`; ArrowDown moves active+focus, Enter sets `selected`+`aria-selected`; empty "No blocked users."; collapse `display:none` (rows mounted); Aria avatar isAi `top:-1px` + status `bottom:-3px` (no overlap); `card-space` no corner badge + inline "🟢 online" intact. **dd-composite tier CLOSED.** Next → the widget tier: `entity-context-menu` (M-RP5.3, the 100% read, uses `status full`) → `temperature-indicator` (M-RP5.4, `meter` via the W-11 dd-socket).

> **M-RP4.9 sampler static-header + scroll + rename ✅ DONE (J-466):** sampler test-bed **ergonomics only** — zero component/registry touch (D-097/D-098). The shell (`app_sampler.svelte` + `app.css`) became a `height:100%` flex column: a **fixed header** block (title + `client|node` skin-swap + the tab bar, `flex:0 0 auto`) over a new **`.sampler-scroll`** body wrapper (`flex:1 1 auto; overflow-y:auto; min-height:0`) holding all five mounted panels — only the body scrolls now (the tab bar no longer scrolls away; `#sampler-root` `overflow:hidden` confines it). Tab labels renamed `DI Atomics · DI Composites · DD Atomics · DD Composites · Widgets` (string-only; the `id` keys are unchanged → N-053 keyed routing + CDP tab-indices unaffected). CDP-verified (9422): five new labels; **registry unchanged at 173** (0 orphans, no delta); `.sampler-scroll.scrollTop=400` while header `getBoundingClientRect().top` constant (`headerFixed:true`) + `docScrollable:false`; DD Composites routing renders `entity-panel`/`entity-item` cells. See N-079. Next → M-RP5.0c (room kind, dd-atomic amend) → the widget tier (M-RP5.3/5.4).

> **M-RP5.0c `room` kind ✅ DONE (J-467):** added **`room`** as a third entity kind — a location peer to `space` (a room/channel inside a space), not a variant of it (`kind:'room'`; `flags.isRoom` rejected). Additive to `EntityDescriptor` + the `entity-avatar` shape branch; **ripples free** through `entity-item`/`entity-panel` with zero code change there (the point of a kind, not a component). **Kind → shape:** identity=circle · space=rounded-square · DM=circle · **room=hexagon** (CSS `clip-path` pointy-top polygon); ring/seed/initials/status inherit. Two `clip-path` consequences (PROVISIONAL, Joe HMR-tunes): the seed border-ring is diminished on the diagonals (the fill carries the shape), and the status badge is nudged onto the lower-right hull (else the clip slices it — decision C). Initials stay centered (decision D). Sampler DD·atomic + DD·composite room cells, CDP-verified (9422): room getter `{kind:"room",…,initials:"GE",seed:hsl(147…)}`; `data-shape="hexagon"` + clip-path applied; initials centered; status nudged on-hull; **ripple** — `entity-item#room-item`/`entity-panel#rooms` avatars all hexagon with no item/panel change; **0-regression** identity=circle / space=square / DM=circle; registry **173→185** (+12), 0 orphans. Next → the widget tier: `entity-context-menu` (M-RP5.3) → `temperature-indicator` (M-RP5.4).

> **M-RP5.0d hexagon badge-clip fix ✅ DONE (J-468):** resolved the M-RP5.0c PROVISIONAL badge-clip via a **fill-layer refactor** (option A). The hexagon `clip-path` lived on the root `<figure>`, clipping every descendant — the `status` corner badge + the isAi `::after` spark were **sliced** (M-RP5.0c only nudged the badge to hide it). Fix: the SHAPE + seed bg/border move onto a new absolutely-positioned inner `<span class="ea-fill">` (`inset:0; z-index:-1`, contained by root `isolation:isolate`); the root `<figure>` is now transparent, un-clipped, `overflow:visible` — initials + badges sit on TRUE box corners. `skin.css` retargets `[data-shape="square"]`/`[data-shape="hexagon"]` onto `.ea-fill`; the hexagon `.status` nudge is removed (badge back to the standard `-3px` corner). CDP-verified (9422): `entity-avatar#room-status` root `clip-path:none` + `overflow:visible`, `.ea-fill` carries the hexagon polygon + seed fill, status badge at the bottom-right corner (`badgeBottomRightCorner:true`, in-viewport, un-sliced); isAi spark `::after` `top:-1px right:-1px` violet on an unclipped root; **0-regression** circle/square/DM fills render (seed bg + 0.8px ring), root `clip-path:none`; registry **185** (`count===unique`), 0 orphans; screenshot `temp/room-hex-fix.png`. Remaining PROVISIONAL (unchanged): the seed ring on the diagonal hull is still absent — a CSS `border` only draws on the rectangular border-box, so `clip-path` cuts bare fill on the diagonals; the fill carries them (Joe HMR-tunes a drawn hull if wanted). See N-081. Next → the widget tier: `entity-context-menu` (M-RP5.3) → `temperature-indicator` (M-RP5.4).

> **M-RP5.3 `entity-context-menu` ✅ DONE (J-469):** the widget tier opens on the dd side — the **SECOND `widget`** (Level-2, D-102) and the **first widget with a real dd-dependency** (the first genuine exercise of the **W-11 dd-socket** — binds an `EntityDescriptor` + a self-status view-model; `core` imports no protocol type). A **gesture-agnostic** droppable overlay: exposes `open(anchor)`/`close()`, the consumer wires the trigger (the reserved avatar/item `onActivate?`, right-click, long-press). Root `<div class="entity-context-menu" role="menu">`, `data-tier="widget"`. **The W-2 behaviour machine (the discriminator, not roving-focus which `entity-panel` already owns):** `closed → open(anchored, focus moved in) → navigating(roving) → dispatch(run handler) → closed`; dismiss = Esc · outside-click · select-then-close · focus-leaves; focus returns to the anchor; W-5 listeners wire on open, tear down on close/unmount (0 orphans). **Header** composes `entity-avatar` + name + `status` **full** (the identity-read surface); item rows = widget-owned `<li role="menuitem">`. Base ships exactly the **universal `identity` item** (kind-labelled; space/room labels + the flag-gated per-(variant,purpose) slots reserved — declared-not-populated, W-8). **Portal-to-body + viewport flip/shift** (opt-in `portal` → `data-portal` → `position:fixed`, relocated to `<body>` to escape ancestor `overflow` clips). Getter G `{open,variant,purpose,kind,itemCount,activeIndex}` (task-state only, no payload — N-060); children self-register `entity-avatar#<id>__avatar` + `status#<id>__status`. **W-3 seam:** action handlers are host-injected callbacks (the `substitutions-editor` `onApply` precedent) — Phase A, no `invoke` in the body. **Two-layer verify green (widget-tier §5):** pure (sampler 9422 — registry entry, open→`{open:true}`, focus-in, dispatch fires `onSelect`, Esc/outside-click dismiss, both accents, 0 orphans; `portal=true` per a **Joe-lock overriding Phase-0 §2 C's inline-sampler provision**, so the sampler demo matches the shell) + effect (real client 9222 — the menu portals out of a real `overflow:hidden` box, `menuTallerThanClipBox` yet in-viewport, the injected `onSelect` reaches real host I/O `invoke('get_state')=DISCONNECTED`, focus-return across the portal, W-5 teardown). Honest notes (Rule 6): ArrowDown **clamps** (the base ships 1 item by design, W-8) and the status-full line **ellipsizes** in a cramped container (cosmetic, HMR). Registry **185→186** (the widget root, closed; +2 `__avatar`/`__status` on open), 0 orphans. Temp client harness reverted (J-428, lean chrome). See N-082. **M-RP5.4 `temperature-indicator` ⏸️ POSTPONED (J-470)** — the last widget-tier item, deferred until fully testable end-to-end. A room/message **activity** widget (heat = conversation activity, materialized as a `meter` fill through the W-11 dd-socket `entity-context-menu` proved real) whose UI representation needs a **`message` component that doesn't exist yet** (registry has no `message`) + a **real room/message activity source** to bind against — both downstream of UI integration; building now = stubs we can't honestly verify (W-8/D-065). Widget tier otherwise complete (`substitutions-editor` ✅ + `entity-context-menu` ✅); resumes when a `message` representation + a live activity feed exist.

> **M-RP6.0 Pre-UI Node↔Client Functional Gate ✅ DONE (J-473):** before the main client UI panel is built, a lean **live** re-verification of the node↔client surface against the *current* binaries — a **D-071 subsystem audit** (client UI panel = dependent milestone; node↔client channel = dependency) and the live-functional slice of the planned Round-2 whole-codebase audit. **Not** a re-run of the closed Multiparty-tests milestone (that stays CLOSED, J-356 — the consolidated ledger was already delivered there; the earlier "MP-R3 ledger owed" note was stale, now corrected). Recommended G-set (single-node, client-facing; federation topology out): **G1** connect+`get_state` · **G2** state sync · **G3** send/receive round-trip · **G4** multi-client/one-node (reuse `MULTIPARTY_S1`) · **G5** client rebind (reuse `MULTIPARTY_S5`). Method: reuse `xgen-mptest` where it covers G1–G5 + live CDP self-drive (9222/9322, J-405); real output quoted (Rule 2). DoD: each G# green + findings + a GO/NO-GO for the client UI panel. Phase-0 `docs/tests/PRE_UI_NODE_CLIENT_GATE_phase0.md` (COMPLETED, v1.0). **Result: all G1–G5 green → GO (J-473).** Harness-primary (`xgen-mptest`, real binaries over `--aicontrol`): G3/G4 = MP-C-01 PASS (19.06s), G5 = MP-C-10 leave&rejoin PASS (22.04s), G1/G2 witnessed by both (connect+register+converge); CDP `get_state` supplementary (J-469). Findings: F-1 no CDP-drivable connect/send pre-UI (client Tauri verb-commands absent, M5 carry-over — closed by M-RP6.1+); F-2 S5 rebind via cross-node MP-C-10. **Next → client UI panel arc (M-RP6.1+)** — assembles the shipped `core`/dd/widget components onto real node↔client state.

> **M-RP6.0d Region / dock model — ✅ DONE (J-474, design-only):** locked the client-UI architecture before any panel build. **Every UI region is a widget** (`kind: system` R1–R8 = non-removable built-ins; `kind: custom` = install/remove, MAY also contribute a region) — the client panel is a **layout of dockable widgets**; the di/dd grid stays the content tier, widgets are the dockable surfaces that host content. One **serializable layout descriptor** (tree of `leaf`/`split`/`tabs`) read by **both** renderers — config-grid (A) now, owned Maya-style dock engine (B) at M-RP7 — so the dock engine is a renderer upgrade, not a region rewrite. A **selection bus** (`{regionId, entity}`) feeds R8 inspector + `entity-context-menu`. Crystallised as **D-103**; widget tier → **v1.2** (+W-12 a widget owns exactly one region, +W-13 system widgets non-removable). Canonical doc `ui/docs/xgen-region-dock-model.md` v1.0. No code. **8 regions:** R1 Spaces · R2 Rooms · R3 Self/connection · R4 Room header · R5 Message stream · R6 Composer · R7 Members · R8 Selection info.

> **🟡 M-RP5.5 `message` component ✅ DONE + M-RP5.6 stream region (PENDING, next-active):** pulled ahead of the live-wiring arc — both build on **sampler fixtures**, no node↔client channel needed, so the honest order is unit-then-container before M-RP6.x. **M-RP5.5** = the `message` **dd-composite** (materializes a protocol message → honest HTML, N-075; composes `entity-avatar`/`label`/`paragraph`/`status`/`led`), a small **type family** (Phase-0 pins v1 vs deferred — v1 (locked): text (author+body+timestamp), system notice; grouped/edited/deleted = fields; deferred: reply/quote, attachment/media, reactions). **M-RP5.6** = **R5 message-stream** as a **system widget** (the listbox-analogue of `entity-panel`: wraps N `message`s + ordering/grouping/day-dividers/scroll). Both sampler-verified. **Phase-0 LOCKED** (`docs/xgen-dd-message-family-phase0.md` v1.0): v1 kinds = `text` (avatar both sides, reserved column, `isOwn` flip) + `system` (no avatar, centered); grouped/edited/deleted = fields; `author: EntityDescriptor` reuse; `details` + `bodyExtras` widget sockets (`WidgetMount[]`; send-status led inside `details`); stream = `core` dd-composite (`role="log"`, click-select), R5 system-widget wrap deferred to M-RP6.x. Steps: **M-RP5.5** A ✅ (`MessageDescriptor` + `text` full; J-478, registry 186→202, feat 166529e) / B ✅ (grouped/edited/deleted; J-479, registry 202→215, feat 063aeab) / C ✅ (`system` kind + full `isOwn` verify; J-480, registry 215→219, feat 09e9cbe — **message family v1 CLOSED**: 2 kinds `text`/`system` + grouped/edited/deleted states + `details` socket); **M-RP5.6** Phase-0 addendum ✅ LOCKED (J-481, `docs/xgen-dd-message-family-phase0.md` v1.1 §9): stream = `core` dd-composite, root `<div role=log>` = scroll viewport, sets each child's `grouped`; grouping = 5-min same-author window (breaks on author/system/day/first, `deleted` keeps author); day-dividers date-carrying (`Today (…)`/`Yesterday (…)`/weekday+date 2–6d/date-only ≥7d), separator row, breaks grouping; scroll = full-A (stick-to-bottom + jump-to-latest pill + preserve-on-prepend, sampler-driven); `background?: WidgetMount[]` persistent fixed layer + `backgroundLive?` switch (default true, settings-binding M-RP6.x), unset+count0 → default paragraph; click-select hook (`selected` $bindable, wiring M-RP6.x); getter G `{count,selected,hasEmpty,groupedCount,dividerCount,atBottom,backgroundMountCount,backgroundLive}`. Steps: **A ✅ DONE (J-482 landed, CDP legs closed J-483)** — `message-stream.svelte` + pure `stream/grouping.ts` (5-min grouping + 4-band day-dividers) + `skin.css` + 5 sampler fixtures; `vite build` clean (161 modules) + 20/20 pure unit test on `grouping.ts`; **CDP legs CDP-verified** (registry `count===unique` 262/262 / 0 orphans both directions / getter-G eight-field / both-accents) once M-RP-CDP1 restored the harness; **registry 219→262 (+43)** / **B** (scroll machine) ✅ **DONE (J-485)** — `atBottom` live (rAF-throttled `scroll` listener, single `BOTTOM_THRESHOLD_PX=80`) · stick-to-bottom on append@bottom / no-yank scrolled-up · jump-to-latest pill (inline chrome, unregistered — `day-divider` precedent) · preserve-on-prepend (`$effect.pre`+`tick()` scrollHeight-delta, first-id heuristic) · mount-to-bottom; Clair wrapped the viewport in a non-scrolling `.message-stream-shell` so the absolute pill anchors to the visible viewport (envelope + `role=log` still ride the inner scroll element — registry root unchanged); no `MessageDescriptor`/`grouping.ts`/`message.svelte` change; sampler `stream-scroll` live fixture (append/prepend/reset). CDP-verified (both accents): registry **262→296 (+34)**, `count===unique`, 0 orphans; mount `scrollTop=398=max`; `atBottom` true→false→true; append stick (444→490) vs no-yank (held 0); jump→max+hide; prepend Δtop=Δheight=68; grouping recompute free (`groupedCount` 7→9); pill accent-neutral / `--accent2` gold↔blue; eye-check OS-screenshot (harness screenshot mode bugged on large PNG, flagged). Feat `5cfd718`. **M-RP5.6 CLOSED** (message dd sub-family: `message` A/B/C + `message-stream` A/B). `temperature-indicator` stays **⏸️ POSTPONED** until the main window is functional (Joe). Next-active = **M-RP5.7** (grouped-avatar suppression, a small correction) → then **M-RP6.1** client UI panel arc.

> **✅ M-RP5.7 grouped-avatar suppression — DONE (J-487):** corrected the M-RP5.5 B grouped-render behaviour — B suppressed the name header but **kept the avatar**, so same-author continuations repeated the identical avatar and grouping read as invisible (Joe flagged it against the M-RP5.6 B screenshot). **Fix:** a `grouped` row (continuation, NOT the group head) renders **neither name NOR avatar** — symmetric; the group head carries both; the avatar grid column stays reserved so bodies stay aligned (no left-shift). **Element absent, not hidden** → grouped cells register neither `__avatar` nor `__name`. Independent of `deleted`/`edited` (grouped is positional). → **D-106**; phase0 **§10** (v1.3). **Feat `fd0af23` (Clair, code-only):** `message.svelte` grouped guard `{#if author}`→`{#if author && !grouped}`; `skin.css` avatar track pinned `auto`→`28px` (`.message` `28px 1fr` / `[data-own]` `1fr 28px`) so the empty continuation cell reserves its column. Scope-clean (no `MessageDescriptor`/`stream/grouping.ts`/`message-stream.svelte`); `vite build` clean (161 modules). **CDP-verified (both accents):** registry **296→286** (−10 = exactly 10 grouped rows × one `__avatar`), `count===unique` (286/286), 0 orphans both directions; 10 grouped rows / 0 leaking avatar or name; text heads keep both (the 3 avatar-less heads are all `kind=system`); no left-shift — head `seed-9` + grouped continuation `seed-8` share grid `28px 311.2px` and body-left 402=402 (`isOwn` `seed-4`@366 = own-side `311.2px 28px`, unrelated); grouped+edited suppresses (grouped+deleted rides the same positional guard); node `#3a7ab0` ↔ client `#c28840`, 286 both; eye-check GREEN (one head avatar + bare continuations, Joe-confirmed). **Doc-wording fix (Clair Rule-6 flag):** §10/D-106 illustrated the grid as `28px 288px`; the real track is `28px 1fr` (288px was never literal) — corrected in-place. **M-RP5.7 CLOSED.** Tighter inter-continuation vertical spacing = deferred skin follow-up (D-065). Next-active = **M-RP6.1** client UI panel arc.

> **✅ M-RP-CDP1 CDP harness restore — DONE (J-483, tooling):** the CDP remote-debug port stopped opening when WebView2 Evergreen auto-updated to **150.0.4078.48** (≥136); the harness had worked unchanged J-405→J-480. **Root cause (corrected):** NOT the Chromium-136 `--user-data-dir` guard (Tauri already forces a non-default data dir on Windows) — **wry overrides the `WEBVIEW2_ADDITIONAL_BROWSER_ARGUMENTS` env var**, so the env-supplied `--remote-debugging-port` never reached the browser command line (confirmed from the real `msedgewebview2.exe` child cmdline). **Fix (D-105):** route the port through Tauri config `additionalBrowserArgs` via a **dev-only `--config cdp.dev.conf.json` overlay** (base `tauri.conf.json` stays port-free → release never exposes CDP). **Universal + verified on all three Tauri apps** — sampler 9422 + client 9222 + node 9322 (harness attach + `__XGEN_DEBUG__` live). Propagation: per-app `cdp.dev.conf.json` + `run-{sampler,client,node}.ps1 -Debug` → `--config`. **Flagged (Rule 6):** `cdp-debug.ps1 -Launch` (built-exe) env-var route stays dead under ≥136 — dev-session attach is the supported path. Unblocked M-RP5.6 A's CDP legs (closed, registry 219→262) + the entire M-RP6.x live-wiring arc.

Feat `96a5a60`. **Next = 6.1k** UI-state store (**its DoD flips `diskette`+`load` to enabled**) · **6.1l** widget manager (**flips `gear`**). **🟢 6.1k — NEXT-ACTIVE. DESIGN LOCKED 2026-07-12 (J-510 → D-114 + D-115); runbook handed to Clair.** **🔑 THE COLLISION WAS REAL AND GROUNDING SETTLED IT:** region-dock §9 specified a **layout-only** store (`xgen-client_layout.json` → `xgen-client_layouts.json`, five verbs, auto at **7.3** / named at **7.6**); surfaces §4 specified a **UI-state** store. **They were never two objects — §9 is the earlier, narrower draft**, written before geometry, the shelf or a named-workspace concept existed. **🔒 D-114: ONE store, `xgen-client_uistate.json`.** §9's identity/reconcile rules survive **verbatim** as the `layout` key's own (`widgetId` durable · drop unknown · re-inject `system` · `version`+migrate); its **filenames, its five verbs and its “layout manager is a widget” line are SUPERSEDED** (region-dock **v1.8**). Auto-save/load **and** named states are both **pulled forward from 7.3/7.6 into 6.1k** — S-8 already locked manual named states as *permanent, not a stopgap*, and the shelf faces dispatch them. **🔑 THE CARVE-OUT — §4's “Rust persists an OPAQUE blob” rule CANNOT hold uniformly, and §4.2 is what breaks it:** the work-area **clamp is mandatory**, and **only Rust can read a work area or apply a rect before the webview exists** — ***a clamp Rust cannot perform is not a clamp***. → **`geometry` = a TYPED Rust struct; every other key = an opaque `serde_json::Value`, round-tripped verbatim.** That is **J-499's rule APPLIED, not excepted**: J-499 rejected `get_layout` because Rust would have **duplicated a type the webview owns**; geometry is the **opposite case** — a type **Rust already owns and the webview cannot**. ***Rust owns what only Rust can do, and stays blind to what the webview owns.*** (Free: an opaque value **preserves unknown keys** → every future key is additive with **no Rust change**.) **⚠️ AND THE FINDING THAT SHAPED THE MILESTONE — grepped, not assumed: FIVE OF THE SIX §4.5 KEYS HAVE NO LIVE SOURCE TODAY.** Only **window geometry** has a feeder (resize grip + native decorations); **grid layout** is a **const**; **shelf favourites** wait on ④ pinning; **collapsed/expanded** has **zero `collapsible` props anywhere in `ui/client`**; **theme** — `theme-*.css` **does not exist** (D-110); **last open room** — no room selection until 6.2. → **6.1k ships `geometry` + `layout` ONLY and RESERVES NOTHING.** An empty `theme: null` key is the ***`tabs`-branch / N-091 shape at file scale*** — *a key nothing writes is a key nobody has round-tripped*; each remaining key lands **with the milestone that creates its source**. **🔒 D-115: M-RP-WINSTATE's criterion FIRES → B** (own it; **no `tauri-plugin-window-state`**), **PHYSICAL px** (N-092b, measured twice — *a rect can only be compared to a work area in the same unit, so a logical rect makes the clamp unimplementable*), clamp-don't-refuse **EXERCISED** (write an off-screen rect, launch), **and ⚠️ N-095's DoD MOVES FROM 7.3 TO 6.1k** — its deferral's premise was that `loadLayout()` **cannot return null**, and **6.1k is the milestone that kills that premise**; leaving it at 7.3 would mean **7.3 closing a hole 6.1k opened**. ***A deferral is valid only as long as its premise holds.*** **RENAMED (2 lines, before the commands existed): `layout.save`/`layout.load` → `uistate.save`/`uistate.load`** — **the store is not a layout**; `layout.*` would be a lie by 6.2. No `saveAs` id (one diskette, one dialog, two outcomes). **Legs, VISIBLE FIRST (Joe's standing brief):** **A** save+load **dialogs** + both commands enter `commandTable` + **`diskette`/`load` flip ENABLED**, in-memory → *the UI is on screen and correctable before any plumbing exists* · **B** Rust `get_ui_state`/`set_ui_state` + the file + `loadLayout()` body swap + **N-095 exercised** · **C** geometry save/restore + **clamp exercised** · **D** named states carry geometry + session key + CDP verify → close. Real client **9222 only** (sampler catalogue must stay **328** — a verify leg); **`cargo test` MUST move** (the honest signal Rust landed). **✅ DEBT CLEARED (Chat's own drift, J-509):** *“Still open for Joe: Settings' own surface”* was **WRONG — D-112 CLOSED it** (Settings takes **`surface: window`**; no `screen` kind was added). **The only thing still open for Joe is top-shelf pinning (surfaces §6 ④) — and it gates nothing.** *A closed decision that keeps advertising itself as open is a record that trains its readers to distrust it.* **✅ 6.1k CLOSED (J-511) — commits `8902efa` + `fdccdb2`.** The store ships: one file `xgen-client_uistate.json`, session + **named** states, **layout + geometry**; **M-RP-WINSTATE absorbed and gone**; `diskette`+`load` **ENABLED** and doing real persistent work; **`gear` is now the LAST disabled face**. **🔑 THE FINDING — A PHASE-LIMIT NOTE IS A COUNTDOWN TOO (N-109):** Leg A shipped a **correct** W-8 honesty note (*“Session-only — not yet written to disk”*); **Legs B/C/D shipped persistence and nobody swept it**, so the milestone reached close-out **telling the user their workspace was not being saved while two named states sat on disk**. Caught on the **painted DOM** at Rule-5 re-drive. ***A stale honesty note is still a false statement — and it is worse than a missing one, because it was written by someone being careful, so the next reader trusts it.*** **The discipline existed and was not generalised:** 6.1j bound *“the disabled face is a COUNTDOWN”* — **a W-8 disclosure is the same object**; the face got swept **because the DoD named it**, the note got missed **because nothing named it**. → **RULE: no milestone closes leaving its own UI asserting a phase-limit it has since removed — sweep the CLAIMS, not just the code; and when a leg ships a disclosure, write its REMOVAL into the DoD of the leg that lifts the limit.** *(4-line fix — the cheapest defect in the milestone and the only one that would have reached a human. Fourth in one family: N-091 · N-097 · N-099 · N-109.)* **D-114 realised literally:** `get_ui_state -> String` / `set_ui_state(json: String)` raw opaque round-trip; **ZERO descriptor types in the Rust crate** (the only `widgetId` is a raw string *inside a test* — **the proof of opacity, not a breach**); `WindowGeometry` the **only** typed part. **D-115 realised:** physical px; clamp **exercised**; **N-095 relocated here and exercised** (corrupt → `DEFAULT_LAYOUT`, **no blank centre** — the J-499 30→21 failure **proven absent**); **`tauri-plugin-window-state` NOT taken**. **RESERVE NOTHING held, visible on disk** — top-level keys are exactly `version`/`session`/`named`/`active`; **D-101 untouched** (config only). **+N-107** (two writers, one file → **read-modify-write on BOTH sides**; ***any format with more than one writer must be MERGED, never REPLACED*** — whole-file serialisation by a narrow writer is data loss wearing a save button's clothes; proven: emptying `named` left `session.geometry` **byte-intact**) · **+N-108** (**THE REGISTRY BREATHES WITH STORE CONTENTS**, not just menu state — the Load picker/Load/Delete sit in `{#if entries.length}`; ***a baseline that depends on a DATA FILE can be wrong on a machine where nothing is wrong***) · **+N-109**. **MEASURED (Chat re-drove every leg; not one number taken on report):** client registry **55 quiescent EMPTY-store** → **59** with a saved state, proven as a **transition in ONE session** (55→59→55, exact return, zero leaks — a number **derived** by arithmetic was refused entry until it had been *seen*); `cargo test` **1517/0/62** (from 1507, **+10**, summed programmatically); `vite build` **165**; `npm test` **41**; **sampler catalogue 328 UNCHANGED** (by scope, both commits); `:modal` true (never the `open` attribute); split `[1,2,7,2]` exact at a **third** distinct width; unknown key **survived the app's own write-back**. **Two legs in NOBODY's matrix, run at verify:** (1) loading a named state with **NO `geometry` key** — the disk had *grown* one (**real data outran the fixtures inside one session**) → window **unmoved**, centre alive, **both guards held** (the J-499 blank-centre trap **designed around and cited by name** before it was ever hit); (2) **Delete is TWO-STEP** (“Confirm delete”) — **S-6's *destruction is deliberate* honoured inside the dialog**, and never in the DoD. **“Static gates need the apps down” is now MEASURED, not folklore:** `cargo test` with the client up dies in 15 s on `failed to remove file …xgen-client.exe` — **the running app holds the binary**. **Deviations (Rule 6):** shell-local `.svelte.ts` store (**the better call** — the exact seam Leg B swapped into) · comboboxes on both dialogs (Joe, mid-flight) · autofocus focus-catcher · **the core `dialog` footer suppressed via `:has()` → its `__close` stays mounted-but-hidden and still registers → FILED, NOT BUILT: a footer snippet slot on the `dialog` core** (**correctly kept out** — a `core` change inside a shell milestone is what makes a registry delta unreadable; its own milestone) · **`quit`-command geometry save — a genuine find made IN VERIFICATION:** `app.exit` (Ctrl+Q / File→Exit) does **not** reliably fire `CloseRequested`, so the final rect would have been **silently lost on that path**. *Found because someone drove the app, not because someone read the code.* **No new D. No new `core`** (dialogs are shell-local → components registry + sampler catalogue untouched). **🟢 NEXT-ACTIVE = M-RP6.1l — the widget manager / plugin list; ITS DoD FLIPS `gear`, the last disabled face.** Under **D-112** it is **ONE pane, TWO entry points** (it lives inside Settings' structure and the shelf gear opens the **SAME** pane — a cut-down popup twin is **explicitly rejected**), it lists **built-ins as `[system]` with Remove disabled** (W-13, Ch6 §6.8.5's badge = W-13 pre-figured), and it is **the home of destruction** (S-6). **Baseline for 6.1l: client registry 55, quiescent, EMPTY STORE — and per N-108 the milestone must STATE which store state it counted in.** **M-RP6.2** R1 Spaces + R2 Rooms on real `KnownSpace`. **M-RP6.3** R6 Composer send verb — *write* half of F-1 (revisits M4 optimistic-ack). **M-RP6.4** R7 Members (resolve M6 `members` data-source shape A/B/hybrid first). **M-RP6.5** R4 heat (`temperature-indicator`) — stays ⏸️ POSTPONED until the main window is functional (Joe); R5 `message` stream built earlier (M-RP5.6).

> **🟢 M-RP6.1l — THE PLUGIN LIST. DESIGN LOCKED 2026-07-12 (J-512); runbook `tasks/M_RP6_1L_PLUGIN_LIST.md` handed to Clair. Its DoD flips `gear` — the LAST disabled face. No new D (a D-112 extension).** **🔑 THE GROUNDING IS THE MILESTONE: THERE IS NO REGISTRY TO ENUMERATE.** Grepped, not remembered: `xgen-common/src/module.rs` ships `Descriptor {kind_id, impl_id, name, assurance}` — **no `host`/`delivery`/`surface` field** (**the vocabulary is shipped; the fields are not**) — and its **only** consumer is `xgen-core/src/dag/store.rs`, **node-side, with no Tauri verb exposing it**; **zero `xgen-module.json` anywhere** (no manifest loader, no `modules/` scan); the **Auth Module registry** and `temperature.rs` are **node-side** and **the client has a verb for neither**; `ui/common/…/widgets/` holds **4 files but the client mounts only TWO** (`substitutions-editor` and `entity-context-menu` are imported **nowhere** in `ui/client`); and **W-13's `kind: system` is a spec word with ZERO code**. **→ 6.1l does not ENUMERATE a registry — it CREATES the first one, in TS, in the frontend, and lists exactly what is real.** ***A list that fakes a universal registry is worse than three honest rows.*** *(Third instance of one shape — J-500's “there is no resident” · J-502's temperature find · this: **a UI milestone cannot manufacture a source that does not exist**.)* **THE LOCK:** **new `ui/common/lib/plugins/registry.ts`** — a typed `PluginDescriptor` + `CLIENT_PLUGINS`, **D-112's three axes (`host`·`delivery`·`surface`) in code for the first time**, **NO RUST** (*J-499's rule applied, not excepted — a plugin descriptor is a type **the webview owns**, the exact inverse of D-114's geometry*) · **`widgetRegistry` DERIVES from it** (a widget is in the grid **because** it is a registered plugin with `surface: region`; the 6 unbuilt regions keep `RegionPlaceholder` — **a placeholder is scaffolding, not a plugin**) · **the pane's own surface is `none`** (Settings takes `surface: window`; the list is **content inside it**, §3.2 — so it **spends no surface** and any host can mount it without lying about what it is) · **🔒 THE SCOPE FORK: SETTINGS DOES NOT EXIST, and 6.1l does not build it** — the pane ships with a **shell-local modal host** (the `about-dialog` precedent) as its **first** entry point, and **`M-RP-SETTINGS` becomes its SECOND mount**, which is *literally* **S-2's “one component, two mounts”**: **nothing built here is thrown away**. *(A grid tile is rejected by **D-112 §9** — dock the plugin manager into a 200px column, then remove the widget that removes widgets; a second Tauri window today is a **frame arc** that would bury the visible part behind plumbing.)* · **THREE honest rows** (`self-panel` · `inspector-panel` · `plugin-list`, **which lists itself**) — **not** the two unmounted widgets, because **registering a plugin the client never instantiates is the unfed-branch shape (N-091)** · **READ-ONLY rows, NO Remove/Disable/Launch/Settings buttons** (no such verb exists — **a permanently-disabled control with no countdown behind it is what 6.1j forbade**, and **J-500's precedent is explicit: the absent slot ships ABSENT, not faked**); what ships is the **`[system]` badge**, which is **Ch6 §6.8.5's own drawing = W-13 made visible**. **S-6 says destruction lives *here*; it does not say it lives here *now*.** · **⚠️ NO W-8 PHASE-LIMIT NOTE ANYWHERE IN THE PANE — an N-109 PRE-EMPT:** a read-only list of what is loaded **cannot go stale**, so there is **nothing to sweep at close**; and if any leg needs a disclosure, **its removal enters the DoD of the leg that lifts the limit, in the same edit that adds it** · **the dialog uses the STOCK `dialog` footer** — reaching for the 6.1k `:has()` hack again is the **2nd independent recurrence** and makes the footer-snippet extraction **its own milestone, never a rider**. **VERIFY:** baseline **client registry 55, quiescent, EMPTY STORE — and the milestone must say so** (N-105/N-108); ⚠️ **the pane's rows register at MOUNT, not on open** (a closed `<dialog>` is `display:none`, not unmounted) → **the new baseline is MEASURED, never derived**; **`cargo test` must stay 1517/0/62 IDENTICAL** (*the inverse of 6.1k's leg — identical **proves** no Rust landed*); sampler catalogue **328 unchanged, by scope**. **LEGS — VISIBLE FIRST:** **A** pane + dialog + `commandTable` + **the gear flips** (hand back for Joe's eyes) · **B** the derive · **C** CDP verify → close.

> 🟡 **M-RP-SETTINGS — the Settings window (Settings as a `surface: window` plugin; the plugin list's 2nd mount). PENDING, filed J-512; gates nothing.** D-112 locked **Settings = `surface: window`, no `screen` kind** — but **there is no Settings window**, and 6.1l deliberately does not build one. This milestone builds it, and it becomes the plugin list's **SECOND mount** — the moment *“one pane, two entry points”* (D-112 / S-2) stops being a plan and becomes true. It is also **`substitutions-editor`'s first client mount** (as **content**, §3.2 — it spends no surface), which is when that widget honestly enters the plugin registry. Scope is a **frame arc**, not a pane: a second Tauri window (own Vite entry, own chrome, own CDP target, own geometry-in-the-D-114-store). **Explicitly foreclosed until reversed by a lock:** the **Discord full-window overlay** shape — that is a fifth surface kind (`screen`), and D-112 says it costs one word in the enum **and must be a lock, never a drift**.

> 🟡 **M-RP-PLUGINS-NODE — the node-plugin read verb (make `host: node` plugins visible to the client). PENDING, filed J-512; Rust/protocol.** The client cannot see a single `host: node` plugin. The storage-engine slot, `TemperaturePlugin` and the **Auth Module** (`delivery: service`) all exist — **node-side, with no verb exposing them to the client**. So M-RP6.1l's list is **honestly client-only**, and says so by omission rather than by a fake section. **This is the M-RP6.6 shape:** *a UI milestone cannot manufacture a source that does not exist* — the enumeration verb is **Rust/protocol work and no part of it may be smuggled into a UI milestone**. **Until it lands, no milestone may render a `host: node` row.**

> **✅ M-RP6.1l CLOSED (J-513) — THE PLUGIN LIST SHIPS. Commit `1dc5849` (6 files, +272/−17). The gear is ENABLED — NO shelf face in the app is disabled. 🟢 NEXT-ACTIVE = M-RP6.2. BACK TO THE GRID.** Shipped: the **first plugin registry in the project** (`ui/common/lib/plugins/registry.ts` — **D-112's three axes in code for the first time**), with **`widgetRegistry` DERIVING from it** (*a widget is in the grid **because** it is a registered plugin with `surface: region`*), plus a **read-only pane** (5th widget, `kind: system`, `surface: none`) in a shell-local modal. **Three honest rows, `[system]` badge, no faked verbs.** **⚠️ CORRECTION 1 — THE MILESTONE WAS BUILT TOO EARLY, AND THE RECORD IS WHY.** 6.1j's countdown said *“no face is enabled before its command exists **and no milestone closes leaving its own face disabled** — 6.1l flips `gear`.”* **Two rules in one sentence:** the guard (**never enable a control that resolves to nothing**) is real; the **schedule** was smuggled in beside it and **pulled a milestone forward because a face was waiting, not because the product needed it**. Joe: *“I honestly thought we would grey the widget manager till we have a working grid.”* → ***A disabled face with a NAMED OWNER is honest indefinitely. A countdown names WHO discharges it — never WHEN, because that turns a guard into a deadline and lets the shelf drive the roadmap.*** **🔒 CORRECTION 2 — J-512's D6 SUPERSEDED (Joe): A GREYED BUTTON IS NOT ONE THING.** Grey because **the verb was never built** = a **dead control** (it lies); grey because **the plugin's own descriptor says so** = **W-13, RENDERED** — *a disabled `Remove` on a `[system]` row **is the information**, and Ch6 §6.8.5 drew exactly that.* → **RULE: every button's state DERIVES from the descriptor and is disabled only for a reason TRUE OF THAT PLUGIN** (`Remove`⇸`system` + confirm for `custom` · `Disable`⇸`system` · **`Launch` only iff `surface==='window'`** — element-absent, not greyed · `Settings`⇸`!hasSettings`, which rides the descriptor, **and the milestone that makes it true owes the button a target in its own DoD**). **MEASURED (Chat re-drove every leg; all of Clair's numbers reproduced):** client registry **67** quiescent/**empty store verified on disk** · gear → **`:modal` true** · G `{3,3,0}` · **67 open === 67 closed**, exact return · painted rows alphabetical + 3×`[system]` + **`btns: 0`** · **the derive proven** (`leafCount:8`/`droppedCount:0`; `self-panel`/`inspector-panel` still real; ratios **`[1,2,7,2]` exact at a FOURTH width**) · accent-neutral (`readable:true`, 0 diffs) · **`cargo test` 1517/0/62 IDENTICAL** → *proves* no Rust · `vite build` **168** · `npm test` **41** · sampler catalogue **328 by scope**. **🔑 New client baseline: 67 (quiescent, EMPTY store) — the next milestone must cite it and state its store state (N-108).** **Deviations, both Chat's:** the runbook's `aria-disabled="false"` literal was wrong (`shelf-face` renders `aria-disabled={disabled || undefined}` — **absent when enabled, never `"false"`**); and **+N-110** — Chat's CDP selectors keyed on `id` when the components key on **`data-debug-id`**, returning **`faces: []`** (*an empty array that looks like a measurement*) and a bare `EVAL ERROR` — ***the N-099 family one layer down: assert the subject is READABLE before asserting anything about it***. **No new D. No new `core`.**

> ⏸️ **M-RP6.1m — THE PLUGIN ROW ACTION SURFACE. POSTPONED (filed J-513, Joe-locked). Four buttons, ZERO live feeders.** The row becomes its own component (a **stacked list**, Ch6 §6.8.5's shape) carrying **`[Settings] [Launch] [Disable] [Remove]`**, every state **derived from the descriptor**. **Deliberately NOT built now** — count the sources: **Settings** (no settings mechanism exists, and per Joe none is being built until the grid works) · **Launch** (no `window` plugin exists) · **Disable** (no disable verb) · **Remove** (no `custom` plugin exists to remove). ***Four controls, four dead branches, nothing verified*** — **precisely the 6.1k finding** (*five of six §4.5 keys have no feeder*) and it takes the same answer: **each control lands with the milestone that creates its source.** **The DESIGN is locked so nobody re-derives it; the BUILD waits.** **At kickoff, mandatory:** a **`custom` descriptor must exist** so **Remove-with-confirm is EXERCISED, not asserted** (N-095's rule — otherwise every button ships in its disabled branch and the milestone has verified nothing). **Recommendation, revisitable:** the row stays **internal to `plugin-list`, not a `core` cell** — one consumer today; the node app's module list is the obvious second, and **that** is when it graduates (D-069 / N-086: *do not add an abstraction with one user*).

> 🟡 **N-007 — THE UNGRADUATED OBLIGATION (filed 2026-06-02; SURFACED 2026-07-12, J-513).** `ui/docs/xgen-ui-notes.md` N-007 says: ***“every module needs a UI representation in both apps — including SYSTEM modules, not only display ones”*** — **install / enable / disable / select** · **status / health** · and a **warnings home** (concretely: the vanilla EventStore's *“storage heavy — install the engine module”* operator contract *“needs a UI home so it isn't only a log line”*). It was written as ***“a first-class question, not an afterthought”*** and marked ***“graduates into Ch6 + the module-framework milestone”***. **It never graduated.** Neither the taxonomy Phase-0 (D-112/D-113) nor the M-RP6.1l design walk cited it, and **the shipped plugin list satisfies roughly a third of it** (it lists; it does not install, enable, select, or report health, and it has no warnings home at all). *A note that files an obligation and is never graduated is a note the project has decided to forget slowly.* **Not a defect in 6.1l** (D-065 — none of those verbs exist). **Owed: a graduation pass into Ch6 §6.8, taken when the module-framework milestone opens — not smuggled into a UI milestone.**

> 🟡 **THE SETTINGS-MECHANISM COLLISION — FILED, DELIBERATELY NOT DECIDED (J-513).** **Two rival answers for what a plugin's settings UI IS, never once put side by side:** **(A)** **Ch6 §6.8.2 / §6.8.5** — `settings_schema`, a JSON-Schema fragment ***“rendered automatically in the module list settings panel”***, opened by the row's `[Settings]` button, *“greyed out if no settings defined”*. **Zero lines of it exist.** **(B)** **surfaces §3.2 + widget-tier** — ***“Settings is a widget, and it hosts other widgets as content”***: the plugin supplies **its own component**, spending no surface. **B is SHIPPED** — `substitutions-editor` (M-RP4.3, **the first widget ever built**) is exactly this. **§6.8 predates what was built, and it loses again — the same species the taxonomy Phase-0 found** (*§6.8 vs everything that was actually built*). **D-112 fixed classification and placement; it never asked how a plugin's settings get DRAWN.** **Joe (2026-07-12): these are visions waiting for real context — *it does not need to be yet another widget system*, and we cannot work on every missing part at once.** **🔒 BINDING: nothing is built toward either mechanism until the widget grid has working regions. No milestone may quietly pick one, and no `[Settings]` button may be enabled until the winner is locked** (the descriptor's `hasSettings` stays `false`, and whichever milestone flips it **owes the button a target in its own DoD**).

> **✅ TAXONOMY PHASE-0 — CLOSED (J-507, 2026-07-12 → D-112 + D-113; `docs/xgen-plugin-taxonomy-phase0.md`).** **🔑 ONE plugin, THREE axes: `host` (node = *system* · client = *ui*) · `delivery` (`compiled` · `service` · `packaged`) · `surface` (`none` · `region` · `shelf` · `window`).** *"Module" and "widget" are not two species* — **and `xgen-common/src/module.rs` (SE-D2) already said so IN CODE, before either spec was written.** **The drift was never Ch6-vs-D-102 — it was §6.8 vs everything that was actually built**, and §6.8 moved (Ch6 **v0.6**). **The slot inventory does NOT retire and was never a rival:** half of it is **regions**, half is **content anchors inside a host widget** — and the anchor mechanism **already ships** (`message.details: WidgetMount[]` + unknown-id drop, W-13): ***`room.message.decorator` is `message.details` under another name.*** **→ ONE placement model + ONE containment model.** **A THIRD species neither spec listed:** the **Auth Module is `delivery: service`** (`AuthModuleXgid` + `endpoint_url` + revocation — **not** the Window-form package §6.8.7 claimed). **Settings = `surface: window`; NO `screen` kind** (surfaces §6 ① CLOSED). **🔒 And D-113 CLOSES Ch6 §6.8.8's widget-sandboxing question, open since April 2026: the boundary is DELIVERY, not "widget"** — a **`packaged` module UI has NO NETWORK (S-1)**, own origin, no Tauri IPC, **never holds the key**, **may not draw trust chrome**, deny-by-default caps — **and S-7: it cannot load at all until that floor ships** (it has **zero lines** today, so the lock costs nothing). **→ M-RP6.1i–l and M-RP7.4 UNGATED.** *Original filing, kept as history:* **A drift surface in the SPECS, not the code.** **Ch6 §6.8.3 already defines three Module UI Forms — Headless · Widget · Window** (D-036) — and **Ch6's “widget” is NOT D-102's widget**: Ch6's is **HTML in an isolated webview** talking to its module backend over a **local WebSocket**, placed by a **named slot** from a fixed inventory (`room.sidebar.bottom`, `room.toolbar`, `global.statusbar`, …); D-102's is a **Svelte component**, in-process, fed by a **`$common` store**, placed by a **dockable region** in the D-103 descriptor. `self-panel`/`inspector-panel` are the latter — no webview, no socket, no slot, no manifest. **→ The project carries TWO PLACEMENT MODELS**, and `xgen-widget-surfaces-phase0.md`'s surface set (`region|shelf|window|none`) is — in hindsight — **a re-derivation of Ch6 §6.8.3 made without consulting it**: they agree on *headless* and *window* and **diverge exactly where placement lives**. **Joe's reconciliation frame (2026-07-11) is the right one:** *“module and widget is the plugin in the two areas: system and ui”* — **one plugin, one list, several UI forms; the work is ALIGNMENT, not a choice between them.** **Must answer:** is a D-102 widget a module, or contributed by one? do the slot inventory and the layout descriptor unify, coexist, or does one retire? does the surface vocabulary need a **`screen`** kind (Ch6 §6.8.5 calls the plugin list *“a screen of its own”*, which `region|shelf|window|none` **cannot express** — this is what blocks **Settings' own surface**)? what does the list render for a **built-in** with no package/manifest/socket (**Joe: listed, distinguished, NO Remove — and Ch6's `[system]`/`[user]` badge is exactly that, W-13 pre-figured**)? **Does NOT block M-RP6.1i/j** (the `shelf` core + mounts are pure UI). **DOES block M-RP6.1l** (the plugin list must list both species) **and M-RP7.4**. Homes: `ui/docs/xgen-region-dock-model.md` §11 · `docs/xgen-widget-surfaces-phase0.md` §9. Spans **D-036 / D-102 / D-103**.

> **🟡 READ / UNREAD MARKERS — A PROTOCOL GAP (PENDING, filed J-503).** **Ch6's UI renders unread counts — and the protocol has no mechanism to produce them.** `RoomListItem` is specified as *“Room name, last message preview, **unread count**”* (Ch6 §6.3) and the Space list carries one too, but **grepping Ch3 AND Ch6 finds no read-marker event.** **A read marker is per-identity state a user expects to FOLLOW THEM TO ANOTHER DEVICE**, so it is **not UI state**: persisting one in `xgen-client_uistate.json` would ship a **local-only marker that never syncs**, and a later protocol read-marker would then be a **second source of truth (D-067, self-inflicted)**. **🔑 And this — not scroll position — is what users actually want on relaunch:** Slack and Discord do **not** restore a pixel offset, they restore you to the **unread line**. **Same species as the J-502 temperature find: a UI chapter drawing a thing the spec never gave it a mechanism for.** **BINDING: no UI milestone may fake it, and none may quietly persist a local marker to make an unread badge light up.** Wants a protocol design pass (Ch3 event shape · per-identity scope · federation semantics · privacy — a read receipt is *observable*, which is a design decision in a no-anonymity network, not a detail).

> **⏸️ `temperature-indicator` — DEFERRED, and its MECHANISM IS WITHDRAWN (J-502).** **⚠️ It is a RENDERER of an existing protocol property, NOT a computation — and the old roadmap wording (“binds `meter` via the W-11 dd-socket”) is WRONG.** Temperature is **spec §3.7.13 (Status: complete)** + **Ch6 §6.12** + **D-061**: reserved `meta_atts` keys `xgen.room_temperature` / `xgen.member_temperature`, a threshold table, buckets `cool|warm|hot|fiery`. **The math is a plugin on the Room's home Node, deliberately outside the protocol**, and Ch6 §6.12.1 states the client treats the values as **opaque** — *“does not attempt to re-derive them”*. Member heat = **accumulated overpass of the Space's own pacing rules** (§3.7.12 / D-060), with visibility already gated by **`member_temperature_visibility: moderator|everyone|self_only`** (§3.7.13.3). **Shipped Rust already exists on both sides:** `xgen-node/src/plugins/temperature.rs` (trait + **`NoOpTemperaturePlugin`**, which returns `None` — **so no node publishes a value today**) and `xgen-client/src/temperature.rs` (`TemperatureUpdate`, `__room__` sentinel, bucket derivation, a `temperature_update` Tauri event, **and the DOM contract `data-temp-state` + `--xgen-room-temperature`/`--xgen-member-temperature`**). **Real shape: a `$common` store + a skin contract, rendered as CONTENT inside other widgets** (room → R2/R4; member → R7/message rows) — **content in a host is not a surface → it spends NO surface and is NOT a dockable panel.** **GATE: live messaging (M-RP6.3 + R5 on live data) AND a non-no-op home-node plugin.** *The second half is a node/plugin arc — the M-RP6.6 shape: **a UI milestone cannot manufacture a source that does not exist.*** **BINDING: no milestone reserves a heat slot or reads a heat store** — R2/R4/R7 ship without it. **Do NOT inherit `meter`/dd-socket at kickoff; re-derive against the shipped contract** (it was *“the first bird”* — named before the widget tier, the dd-socket, the region model **or** Ch6 §6.12 were consulted; **the deferral is precisely why it never hardened wrong**).

> 🔒 **THE DOCK ENGINE (RENDERER B) — PHASE-0 LOCKED 2026-07-12. `docs/xgen-dock-engine-phase0.md`.** Renderer A (`region-shell`, M-RP6.1f) renders a **const** tree into flex boxes and **nothing mutates it** — *it looks like a dock and is a picture of one*. Renderer B turns it into one. **A renderer upgrade, not a region rewrite** — both renderers read one D-103 descriptor. **§0 — Joe's autonomy grant: Chat holds full authority over every MECHANICAL aspect of this arc — EXCEPT THE GRAPHICAL APPEARANCE OF THE CREATED ELEMENTS, WHICH IS JOE'S** (and per N-090 appearance is **wide**: gaps, spacing, sizing, tracks, layout — so stripe height, grip size, seam thickness, band thickness, the folded form and the hole raster are all Joe's).

> 🔒 **D-116 — THE DOCK REARRANGES; IT NEVER JOINS. LOCKED (Joe, 2026-07-13).** **A target tile is an ADDRESS, not a container.** The tree's only vocabulary is **relative** — *above that one · below · left · right*. **The drag verb is not "put A into B"; it is "insert A here, and *here* is an EDGE of B".** → **no centre drop-zone** (a tile's centre is **inert**) · **no tabs, ever produced** · **no docked/undocked mode** (*a region is docked iff it appears in the tree* — a **query**, not a flag) · **no `M-RP-ROVING` prerequisite** (a tab strip would have been roving's 5th impl; not built → stays filed). ***Tabs is what you reach for when you don't have fold. We have fold.*** **The door stays SHUT, not LOCKED:** `types.ts` still types `tabs`, `resolve.ts` still drops it with a warn — zero cost, zero schema change if it is ever wanted. **⚠️ Re-opening tabs is an EXPLICIT act; it never arrives as a rider on a drag milestone.**

> **✅ M-RP7.1b — the fold axis becomes the user's choice; splits shrink-wrap; the hole gets a floor — CLOSED (J-516).** `0f25e50` + `14eb4d8` [Clair] + the appearance fix [Chat]. Phase-0 → **v1.4**. **① Two fold buttons** — `collapsed` is now a **DIRECTION** (`'width' | 'height'`), **stored** (*user intent is not derivable from anything*); the **along/across mode** stays derived. **② 🔒 The split SHRINK-WRAP:** all children folded **across** ⇒ the split collapses and **returns its weight to its siblings by the `flex` they already have**. **Measured: the left column 215px → 22px, freed 2/12 redistributed at EXACTLY `1:7:2`, hole 0.** **The descriptor is never mutated** — weights are ignored while folded and honoured again on unfold. **Mixed folds deliberately do NOT shrink-wrap** (verified reachable, and kept). **③ 🔒 Holes are legal, painted, INERT** — **a hole is NOT a drop target** (a target tile is an ADDRESS; a hole has none). **🔑 THE MIGRATE RAN, FOR THE FIRST TIME EVER.** Grounded first: **`migrate` DID NOT EXIST** — only comments; `version` bumped **twice** with **no function**. *A path described in three documents and implemented in none is not a path; it is a plan.* **`migrateLayout` created**, six vitest cases fed with hand-built `v2` trees, **and DRIVEN LIVE through the real Load dialog** (Clair's hand-back said that was impossible; **the way in was three clicks**). **A migrated `v2` layout is HOLE-FREE BY CONSTRUCTION** (the old derived rule only ever folded ALONG), and **the migrate never rewrites the stored state** — a read-path transform, idempotent, non-destructive. **⚠️ THE DEVIATION WAS CORRECT AND THE RUNBOOK WAS WRONG:** `migrateLayout(raw, fallback)` — `resolve.ts` is `core`, `DEFAULT_LAYOUT` is shell-local; the runbook would have made **`core` import the client's default**, a second source of truth (**D-067**, the J-499 drift). **🔒 CONVENTION A** — *the chevron always points where the region will GO if you press it*. **⚠️ "Convention B" was NEVER true of the build:** the **width** button was always A; only the **height** button spoke disclosure — from Joe's original `[<][V]` sketch, **which mixes the two conventions in one stripe**. **The fix was two rotation values swapped.** → **N-116: the CHAPTER was self-consistent and the CODE was not — re-reading would never have caught it.** **MEASURED (Rule 5):** registry **67** (quiescent · empty store · **no selection** · nothing folded · **zero saved UI states**) · `cargo test` **1517/0/62 IDENTICAL** (56 terminators, run twice) · `npm test` **49** · `vite build` **168** · sampler catalogue **328** · ratios `[1,2,7,2]` exact at **1291px**. **⚠️ N-115 — A FOURTH REGISTRY AXIS, AND IT LIVES ON DISK: ONE SAVED UI STATE = +4** (the Load picker + its two buttons, **element-absent when there is nothing to pick** — correct per J-500). **→ `67` is only true with ZERO saved UI states; one diskette click and the client reads 71 forever.** **And `71` is now AMBIGUOUS — selection-active = 71; zero-selection + one-saved-state = 71.**

> ### ✅ **M-RP7.2 — SPLITTER RESIZE ON THE SEAM — CLOSED (J-519). Code `9faa38c` [Clair], 8 files, +512/−28. Zero Rust · zero sampler · zero node** (diffstat + the identical `cargo test`, **not asserted**). `mutate.ts` **born** (`resizeSplit`, pure) · `path: number[]` threaded · the **`.region-seam` ELEMENT** replaces the flex `gap` (a `gap` cannot be hit-tested) · pointer-capture gesture · `resolve.ts` exports `isFoldAlong`/`splitShrinkWraps`/`carriesMainAxisWeight` — **one source for tile fold-mode, split shrink-wrap AND seam liveness.** **🔑 THE CENTRAL CLAIM, PROVEN — and only provable because leg 0 built the instrument first: MID-DRAG (button still down) the descriptor still read `[1,2,7,2]` while the tile had painted 74→176px; AFTER release `[237,63,700,200]`** — sum 1200, pair total invariant, **untouched siblings exactly ×100**, no jump on commit. **MEASURED (Chat re-drove every leg, Rule 5):** registry **67** (quiescent · empty store · no selection · nothing folded · zero saved states; **the seam does NOT register**; returns to exactly 67 after a full churn) · `cargo test` **1517/0/62 IDENTICAL** · `npm test` **59** · `vite build` **169** · **`--region-min` reads `22px` ON THE SEAM** (Chromium resolves the nested `var(--region-stripe)` in a computed custom property — the runbook's central open question) · **7 seams** · clamp stops at **exactly 22px** with `data-collapsed`/`data-fold-mode` **null** — ***it stops, it does not fold*** · shrink-wrap: both adjoining root seams `false`, **the column's own internal seam `true`** (across-folded leaves keep their height weight — the nuanced case, out of ONE predicate). **⚠️ The handed-over client had a SELECTION ACTIVE — a count read in that state is 71, not 67 (N-112);
