PAINT ORDER decides WHICH.*** **Clair's `z-index:1` deviation is CORRECT and ACCEPTED**, and **M-RP7.4's drop bands inherit the same trap.** ② the §5 premise was **misattributed** — she exported the predicates and **deduped three call-sites instead of adding a fourth.**

> ### ✅ **M-RP7.2b — THE REGION-OWNED GAP MODEL — CLOSED (J-523). `skin.css` ONLY — no component, no descriptor, no vitest, no Rust.** **`--region-gap: 4px` is the ONLY spacing token; `--region-pad` and `--region-seam` are DELETED.** Every tile carries `margin: G` · every split carries **nothing** (and must never paint again) · every seam is a **ZERO-WIDTH** flex child with `−G/2` each side — its box lands **dead centre of the gap**, so the drag survives. `--region-seam-hit` re-derived to `max(4px, calc(var(--region-gap) / 2))`: the seam is now always zero-width, so the `::before` **is** the grab target (≥ 8px, and ≥ the visible gap). **⚠️ ZERO VISUAL DELTA — AND ITS JUSTIFICATION WAS A GHOST (N-124): "tile↔hole is 0" IS NOT OBSERVABLE**, because since J-521 a hole and a gap are the **same surface**. A/B-measured against the old geometry injected live: insets 4/4 vs 4/4, distance-to-hole 774.8 vs 774.8, gap census 4 everywhere under **both**. **It shipped on the honest reason: it deletes a MECHANISM, not a token** (perimeter-as-padding + gap-as-seam-thickness = two mechanisms that had already drifted apart once), **and a gap that lives on the REGION travels with it when M-RP7.4 moves it.** **⚠️ N-124a — two arc baselines were overstated: `[1,2,7,2]` is really `2.000112` (and the OLD geometry gives `2.000114` — which is what PROVES no bias), and the clamp stops at `22.29px`, not "exactly 22".** MEASURED: perimeter 4/4/4/4 · 14 adjacent tile pairs all exactly 4 · MID-drag `[1,2,7,2]` untouched, AFTER `[194,106,700,200]` · grab zone 9/9/20px at gap 0/4/20 · registry **67** · `npm test` 59 · `vite build` 169.

> ### 🔑 **N-120 — THE ONE DEFECT M-RP7.2 SHIPPED. Clair filed it "latent, out of scope"; Chat REACHED it; it INVERTS A RESIZE. → A REQUIRED LEG OF M-RP7.3.** `region-node` threads `path` from the **RESOLVED** child index; `resizeSplit` walks the **DESCRIPTOR** by it — and **`resolve.ts` DROPS** (unknown leaves · `tabs` · all-dropped splits). **Any drop and the index spaces diverge.** **Reached, not argued:** plant ONE unknown `widgetId` → 3 descriptor children, **2 tiles paint, 1 seam** → drag it **right to ENLARGE `spaces`** → `[1,1,1]` → **`[1660,340,1000]`**: **`spaces` HALVED (660→335px), `rooms` GREW.** ***The resize did the exact opposite of the gesture*** — the pair resized was `ghost ↔ spaces` — **and 55% of the row's weight now belongs to a widget that does not exist**, which **M-RP7.5 would write to DISK.** **🔒 Proven in the same poisoned layout: FOLD IS DROP-SAFE (addresses by `regionId` — an IDENTITY); RESIZE IS DROP-FRAGILE (addresses by `path` — a POSITION).** ***A position is only an address in the tree it was counted in.*** **🔑 The rule, bigger than this arc: A DERIVED VIEW MAY RENUMBER — an index into a derived view is NOT an address into the source.** *`resolve.ts` already knows the descriptor index at the moment it drops — it just throws it away.* **⚠️ Unreachable in today's build** (all 8 ids registered; `tabs` never produced — D-116) — **reachable the first time a widget id is retired or renamed and a user loads a saved workspace** (the W-13 reconcile case; the Load dialog is three clicks away). ***"Unreachable today" has now been the wrong argument FIVE times here*** (N-091 · N-097 · N-099 · N-109 · N-116). **→ M-RP7.3 opens with it: a misaddressed `resize` nudges two integers; a misaddressed `move` RELOCATES A PANEL INTO THE WRONG BRANCH.**

> **⚠️ THE M-RP7.1 SE CORNER TRIANGLE IS STILL PAINTED-DEAD AND ITS DISCHARGER SAID "M-RP7.2".** This milestone resized on the **seam**, not the **corner**. **RE-POINTED, not quietly dropped** → **`M-RP-SKIN` decides whether a corner grip exists at all; if it stays, it needs a milestone that makes it LIVE.** *A countdown whose milestone came and went without discharging it is the painted-dead chrome this project keeps refusing.*

> **🎛️ THE GRID'S SPACING — ONE TOKEN, `--region-gap: 5px` (Joe, 2026-07-14; J-520).** **⚠️ J-517 shipped TWO tokens at TWO values** (`--region-seam: 1px` between tiles, `--region-pad: 6px` at the frame) — **Joe found it by looking at the screen** and drew two arrows on a screenshot: *the regions were glued to each other and floating off the frame.* ***A gap that means one thing at the edge and another between tiles is not a knob; it is a bug with two names.*** Both now **derive** from `--region-gap` and must never drift apart again. **RE-DRIVEN: edge 5 · seam 5 · tile-to-tile 5 · grab zone 8px · registry 67 · MID-drag descriptor still `[1,2,7,2]`, AFTER `[198,102,700,200]`** — behaviour-neutral. **`--region-seam-hit` 3→1px** (it was sized for a 1px seam; against a 5px one it reaches into the neighbours' edges). **🔒 JOE'S REGION-OWNED GAP MODEL IS RIGHT AND IS NOT WHAT SHIPPED** — *"the gaps are part of each region, transparent, like a margin; **they overlap when they meet, not added**; 5px (not 10px) between regions AND between region and edge."* **⚠️ That is TRUE MARGIN COLLAPSING, and FLEXBOX DOES NOT COLLAPSE MARGINS — they ADD.** **The result is still exactly reachable: every TILE takes a full margin `G`, splits take NOTHING, and the seam becomes a ZERO-WIDTH flex child with `−G/2` each side, cancelling the double** → tile↔tile **G** · tile↔edge **G** (**`--region-pad` DISAPPEARS — the edge gap BECOMES the region's own margin**) · tile↔**hole** **G** (**today ZERO**) · **nested composes at any depth** · **and the seam's zero-width box lands DEAD CENTRE, so the drag survives.** **⚠️ — AND THE NEXT SENTENCE WAS WRONG; SEE THE RETRACTION BELOW (J-521).** *Chat wrote:* **"IT IS A DEPENDENCY, NOT A PREFERENCE: `.region-split` PAINTS today, so under this model a nested split's background would paint INTO its neighbour's margin and eat the gap. The model REQUIRES transparent splits — which IS `M-RP-PLATE` → they are ONE MILESTONE."** **✗ The premise was true and the conclusion was not: making the splits stop painting is a two-line skin move, not the plate widget. The blocker never existed.** **What shipped is an honest DOWN-PAYMENT** — the two boundaries Joe can SEE are uniform; the third is still zero **and is stated in the skin, not hidden.**

> **⏸️ `M-RP-RESTART` — FILE ▸ RESTART **AND** REVERT. FILED (Joe, 2026-07-14; J-520). LANDS WITH M-RP7.5.** Joe: a File ▸ Restart item, *"disabled in states where it cannot be executed — no problem for me."* **The guard is right; grounding split the item in TWO.** **`Restart`** (bounce the process) is **real today** — **`tauri-plugin-process` is ALREADY a dependency** and *is* `restart()`/`exit()` — **⚠️ WIRED + GRANTED SINCE M1** — **N-116 CORRECTED (J-528): `.plugin()`-wired at `desktop.rs:543`, `process:default` granted at `capabilities/default.json:8` (`c23c06a`). The "declared but never wired" claim was record-vs-code (Clair caught it, Rule 6); Leg C added ZERO Rust.** **⚠️ `Revert`** (*"no automatic save on exit, load last automatic save"*) **has no automatic save to revert to — the grid does not write `session.layout` until M-RP7.5.** → **"restart without saving" and "restart" are the SAME THING today, and `Revert` would be greyed 100% of the time.** ***A permanently-disabled item is not a guard; it is a promise*** — the painted-dead chrome this project keeps refusing, and **the same reason M-RP7.6's grid lock cannot ship early.** **→ both ship LIVE, together, with M-RP7.5.** **⚠️ Named now: after M-RP6.3 a restart tears down a live WS that may hold UNSENT MESSAGES.**

> ### 🔑 **THE BACKDROP MOVED: `.region-split` → `.region-shell` (J-521) — AND THE "DEPENDENCY" ON `M-RP-PLATE` IS RETRACTED.** Joe, after J-520 made the gap uniform: ***"the not-transparent padding around whole grid is still there."*** **He was right, and the WIDTH was never the problem — the SURFACE was. MEASURED:** `.region-split` painted `--s5` + the raster, while **`.region-shell` AND `.app-center` were BOTH transparent** → ***the gap BETWEEN regions showed the GRID's backdrop; the gap AROUND the grid showed THE ROOM BEHIND THE GRID.*** **Two different surfaces for one conceptual gap.** *His phrase — "the edge gap is made from outside" — described the implementation exactly, from the picture alone.* **🔒 NOW the SHELL paints · the SPLITS are TRANSPARENT · the TILES are OPAQUE ⇒ every gap (seam · hole · perimeter) is ONE SURFACE.** **RE-DRIVEN: shell `rgb(52,59,71)`+raster · split transparent · edge gap 5 · seam 5 · registry 67 · MID-drag descriptor still `[1,2,7,2]`, AFTER `[176,124,700,200]`.** **🔒 AND THIS IS ALREADY THE `M-RP-PLATE` ARCHITECTURE, arrived at early and for free** — that milestone now only swaps a `background-image` for a real element. **⚠️ THEREFORE THE RETRACTION: J-520 claimed the region-owned gap model was GATED on `M-RP-PLATE` "because it requires transparent splits". WRONG. It requires the splits not to PAINT — which is this two-line skin move, NOT the plate widget.** *Chat conflated "splits must not paint" with "the milestone that makes splits not paint"; the plate is a SUPERSET, not a prerequisite.* **🔒 THE BLOCKER IS GONE — the model is buildable now** (tile `margin: G` · seam zero-width with `−G/2` · delete `--region-pad`), **before or after M-RP7.3; Joe's call.** **⚠️ THREE-FOR-THREE THIS ARC: N-118's two wrong-but-coherent diagnoses, and now a wrong-but-coherent DEPENDENCY carrying a 🔒.** ***A dependency you have not TRIED TO BREAK is a dependency you have ASSUMED.***

> **🎨 THE REGION'S OWN EDGE — `--region-border: 1px` · `--region-edge: var(--s4)` (Joe, 2026-07-14; J-522).** Joe: *"give each region 1px very fine smooth border. **When gap is 0, there is no sense of regions, all is melted.**"* He tested the extremes (0 and 20) and settled on **`--region-gap: 4px`**. **⚠️ THE COLOUR COULD NOT BE `--s5` — J-521 MADE `--s5` THE BACKDROP**, so an `--s5` edge is **invisible at any gap > 0**. The edge must read against **both** neighbours — darker than the backdrop, lighter than the tile body — and **`--s4` does both.** *A token that was correct for a hairline stopped being correct the moment the surface behind it changed.* **🔑 N-121 — A REAL `border` BROKE THE ARC'S BASELINE, AND IT WAS MEASURED BEFORE IT WAS BELIEVED: `[1,2,7,2]` came back `[1, 1.97, 6.90, 1.97]`.** With `flex: n 1 0`, flex hands out the **FREE** space by weight and then each item's **BORDER BOX = content + 2×border** — **a constant added to every tile.** Content boxes stay proportional; **border boxes do not — and every rect JS can measure is a border box.** **⚠️ WORSE THAN COSMETIC: the splitter derives its drag fraction from the neighbours' `getBoundingClientRect()` widths — a border would have biased the resize arithmetic by `2×border` per tile, systematically and silently, and nothing would ever have failed.** **✅ `outline` + `outline-offset: -1px`: identical hairline, ZERO layout impact → re-measured `[1,2,7,2]` EXACT**, `--region-min` still 22, folded strip 22px / overflow 0, clamp still stops at 22 without folding. *(Inset `box-shadow` rejected — it paints BELOW the children and the stripe's `--s2` would cover the top hairline.)* **🔑 N-122 — AND JOE'S OWN EXTREME TEST HAD A HOLE HE COULD NOT SEE: at `--region-gap: 0` the SEAM IS ZERO PX WIDE** (the seam element *is* the gap), and `--region-seam-hit` was a constant 1px → **a 2px grab target: alive, and ungrabbable.** *He verified it did not break; he did not verify he could still drag it.* **✅ The hit area now COMPENSATES for the gap** — `max(1px, calc((8px - var(--region-gap)) / 2))` → **a constant grab zone: MEASURED 9px @ gap 4 · 9px @ gap 0 · 22px @ gap 20, and the drag COMMITS at gap 0.** ***A knob that silently kills a gesture at one end of its range is not a knob.*** **⚠️ `calc()` is safe there ONLY because that token is never read by JS** — `getComputedStyle` does not resolve `calc()` in a custom property, and `parseFloat` on it yields **`NaN`**; **`--region-min` and `--region-snap` ARE read by JS and must stay plain values.**

> ### 🟢 **✅ M-RP7.3 — the mutation algebra (pure) — CLOSED (J-524).** `mutate.ts` is now the complete algebra: `resizeSplit` (**N-120 fixed** — a two-index descriptor pair, `srcIndex` on every resolved node), `foldLeaf` (migrated verbatim out of the shell), `move` (remove → collapse-degenerate → insert; **no re-normalise pass — it was overstated work, deleted**). **N-120 reached not argued (V1): the poisoned-layout seam drag GREW `spaces` and left the ghost byte-identical.** **Move surfaced N-125** — the renderer's index-keyed `{#each}` reused tiles across regionIds (registry 67→65); fixed with stable node-identity keys. **✅ M-RP7.4 — drag to dock — CLOSED (J-525).** The dead grip ACTIVATED; a user rearranges the grid by hand. **ONE grid-level overlay (D1)** designs the N-119 paint-order class out; the band is chosen by `elementFromPoint`→`data-edge` (**D2**, never quadrant math); a hole offers no band (**D3**, unsayable); a no-op band never highlights because D4 reads **`isMoveNoop` — the SAME predicate `move` commits on** (N-126). No new tree algebra — the gesture calls the live `handleMove`. Every leg re-driven with the trusted-pointer harness (V1 the hit-test sweep `top:stream/bottom:stream/left:stream/right:stream/center:-`; V2 a region relocating far-left→right-of-stream; V3 registry 67/stampMismatches 0 through a drag sequence). **Zero Rust · zero sampler · no schema change** (`cargo test` **1517/0/62 IDENTICAL**; `npm test` 77 · `vite build` 169). `M-RP-MOVE-KBD` filed. **✅ M-RP7.4a — the division preview — CLOSED (J-526).** Split M-RP7.4's `bands` into hit targets (byte-identical) + a new `.region-drop-preview` = the drop-half of the hovered tile (`move`'s 50/50); `pointer-events:none`, guard just `drag.edge` (no-op/hole free). **⚠️ FINDING N-127: the preview is half the target AS IT IS NOW, not the post-drop rect — `move` reflows the grid, so the rendered rect is ~40–100px off; Joe accepted the DIRECTIONAL preview ("i think it works as i want"), exact version filed `M-RP-PREVIEW-EXACT`.** `region-shell.svelte`+`skin.css` only; `cargo test` 1517/0/62 IDENTICAL. **✅ M-RP7.4b — the exact preview — CLOSED (J-527).** Computes the preview from a **dry-run of `move`** (pure → zero side-effect, V6-proven) + `resolveLayout` + a weight-proportion walk mirroring `flex: {weight} 1 0` + the shrink-wrap strip-exclusion (`carriesMainAxisWeight` reused). **The reflow is FIXED — `spaces`→`stream` right left 60px → 2px.** **⚠️ N-128: the runbook's ≤2px floor was a single-level measurement; the multi-level path to a tile accumulates ~2px/level of gap (up to ~14px on a 3-level wrap). Joe reset the bar to OPTICAL correctness — met; floor recorded, §5 offscreen-render stays filed.** `region-shell.svelte` only; supersedes `M-RP-PREVIEW-EXACT`. **🟢 NEXT-ACTIVE — M-RP7.5 — the session layout feeder — ✅ CLOSED (J-528), feeder shipped, ZERO Rust. (Historical pointer; the grid now WRITES `session.layout`; §12 — N-107's per-key merge inside `session`; then M-RP7.6 grid lock · M-RP7.7 node inherits the frame + grid).

> **🔒 M-RP7.2 — THE EIGHT DESIGN LOCKS (taken under §0 autonomy: mechanics are Chat's; only graphical appearance is Joe's).** **L1 — `mutate.ts` is BORN at 7.2** (`resizeSplit` only); **7.3 adds `move` and pulls `fold` out of the shell.** *⚠️ The arc table put the algebra AFTER the first mutation, and that cannot be true: **the first mutation IS algebra.*** **L2 — INTEGERS ONLY** (§7 bans floats): resolution comes from an **exact integer scale-up** — `[1,2,7,2]` → ×100 → `[100,200,700,200]`, then **only the dragged pair moves**; **untouched siblings keep their proportions to the byte** and the pair's total is invariant, so nothing else shifts. *Cost, stated: a saved workspace reads the scaled numbers after a drag — the price of §7's own lock.* **L3 — LIVE PREVIEW; integers written ONCE on `pointerup`** (no float ever reaches the descriptor; the proof is ONE clean diff, not sixty). **L4 — a seam is draggable iff BOTH neighbours carry a main-axis weight** — one derived predicate, and folded-along + shrink-wrapped fall out of it with **no special case**. **L5 — `path: number[]`, DERIVED — NO schema change** (a split id would be a key nothing round-trips, the M-RP6.1k finding); **M-RP7.4's `move` needs the identical addressing, so it is paid once.** **L6 — the min-clamp READS THE SKIN** (N-090); **it STOPS, it never auto-folds** (§4.2). **L7 — NO keyboard resize** (it would put a tab stop on **all 7 seams** nobody asked for, *and* dodge the harness this milestone exists to land). **L8 — THE SEAM DOES NOT REGISTER** (`.region-split` has no getter either — the seam is its chrome): **registry stays 67**; proof is the tree diff + painted geometry. **⚠️ TWO RETRACTIONS, BOTH CHAT'S:** **over-asked** — three questions to Joe, two of them Chat's own to answer under §0 (*autonomy is not a licence to be vague; it is an obligation to decide*); and **named a scrollbar-collision risk WITHOUT MEASURING IT** (rule ③ cuts both ways — **do not name a risk you have not grounded**).

> **⏸️ `M-RP-SKIN` — THE APPEARANCE PASS (FILED 2026-07-13, Joe: *"majority of graphical elements will be changed or updated after ui mechanics completion"*).** **🔑 The NAMED DISCHARGER for every `PROVISIONAL` marker in the grid arc** — the fold chevrons, stripe/grip/triangle sizing, the folded strip's form, **`--region-pad` / `--region-seam`**. **The countdown rule satisfied at ARC scale: WHO = Joe, WHICH MILESTONE = `M-RP-SKIN`.** *Not a deferral — **you cannot tune an appearance whose mechanics are still moving underneath it.*** **⚠️ SKIN ONLY** (N-090/N-025); **if it needs a component change, that is a FINDING, not a licence.** **⚠️ ONE EXCEPTION (2026-07-14): the hole raster's discharger is `M-RP-PLATE`, NOT this** — that milestone **replaces** it rather than tuning it.

> **⏸️ `M-RP-PLATE` — THE GRID BACKDROP: AN INERT, LIVE-SWITCHABLE PLATE WIDGET UNDER THE TILES. FILED 2026-07-14 (Joe).** *"The space under regions aka 'the hole' will be customizable — from solid black to animated reactive colour fractal clouds. What is in the hole right now is a **dev plate with no background defined**. The real one we do by a **background widget** which sets it by its own setting."* **🔑 THE MECHANISM ALREADY SHIPS, ONE LEVEL DOWN** — `message-stream` carries **`background?: WidgetMount[]`** (an **array** → a *stack* of plates), `inset:0`, **`pointer-events:none`**, W-13 unknown-id drop, and a **`backgroundLive`** switch (*"a reactive widget renders frozen when false; a static object ignores it"*), locked J-481 / shipped J-482 — **chat wallpaper. This is grid wallpaper: the SAME SEAM.** *"Solid black" is a `div` with a colour; "fractal clouds" owns a canvas; the host never learns the difference.* **🔑 IT COSTS NO SURFACE** — W-12 says at most one of `region·shelf·window·none`, and a backdrop declares none of them: **content inside a HOST is not a surface** (surfaces §3.2 — the `temperature-indicator` ruling). **No tile, no region, no shelf face, no new surface kind.** **🔑 A HOLE CANNOT HOLD ANYTHING** (no element, no address) → **the plate is a BACKDROP, not a hole-filler: ONE plate behind the whole `.region-shell`; the tiles are OPAQUE, so what shows through IS the holes** — and the `--region-pad` perimeter with them. **🔒 IT MAY READ THE POINTER; IT MAY NEVER CAPTURE IT** (`pointer-events:none`) — *the instant a hole is clickable it has an ADDRESS and D-116 falls.* **⚠️ IT RETIRES §4.5's "zero new DOM, one skin rule"**: a CSS background cannot do a canvas or anything reactive, so the dynamic plate **is an element** — today's raster is the placeholder it **replaces**, not the seed it grows from (and the raster is **promoted, not deleted**: it becomes the first *system* plate widget, so the socket ships **FED**, D-065/N-091). **⚠️ GATE, AND IT IS A LOCK NOT A PREFERENCE: there is no settings mechanism, deliberately** — J-513 filed the settings collision as **explicitly undecided**, binding: *nothing is built toward either until the grid works*. **Joe: *"that is why i don't want to solve it now. now has priority widget grid with functional empty regions."*** **🔒 THIS ARC RESERVES NOTHING FOR IT** — no prop, no descriptor key, no store. **Zero impact on M-RP7.2.**

> **⚠️ CLIENT REGISTRY BASELINE: 67 — AND FOUR AXES, NOT ONE.** The number is only readable if the state it was counted in is stated: **quiescent** (N-105 — menus/popups register children while open) · **store contents** (N-108) · **selection state** (N-112 — a selection adds 4; a fold removes the folded body) · **🔑 named-UI-state count (N-115 — ONE saved UI state adds 4, AND IT PERSISTS TO DISK).** **`67` = quiescent · empty store · no selection · nothing folded · ZERO saved UI states.** **⚠️ `71` IS AMBIGUOUS — it is both "selection active" and "one saved UI state".** *Every milestone cites the baseline AND names the state it counted in, or it has recorded a number, not a measurement.*

> ⚠️ **RULE 8 (CLAUDE.md, 2026-07-12, Joe) — A MILESTONE ID NEVER TRAVELS ALONE.** Every designation is written **`ID — short title`**, everywhere: chat, runbooks, JOURNAL, CLAUDE.md, this file, commit subjects, kickoffs. Not `M-RP6.2` but **`M-RP6.2 — R1 Spaces + R2 Rooms on real KnownSpace`**. ***A bare ID is a token you have to look up; a title is a thing you can object to.*** **Filed after M-RP6.1l — the plugin list**, whose shape was only visible **ex-post, once it had shipped** — and by then an objection costs a milestone instead of a sentence. **The title is what makes disagreement possible BEFORE the work exists**, which is the entire point of a design walk. **Historical entries are NOT rewritten** (the J-001–J-064 agent-name posture: the record is the record).

> 🟡 **M-RP-ROVING — extract the roving-tabindex helper (PENDING; its OWN milestone, NEVER a rider). D-069's four-recurrence bar is MET.** Roving tabindex is now implemented **four independent times** — `entity-panel` · `menu-bar` · `menu` · `shelf` (the fourth, M-RP6.1i, where it was **copied deliberately rather than extracted mid-arc**). **D-069's bar for promoting a pattern is four, and it has been reached**, so the extraction is now owed. **Scope:** one shared `$common` helper (the `Accelerator`/`KeymapRegistry` shape — a pure, DOM-lean value-object, vitest-verified), then **four call sites migrated one at a time**, each re-verified in the surface that owns it (sampler 9422 for the `core` trio; real client 9222 for the shelf). **⚠️ It touches FOUR closed, verified components** — which is exactly why it must never ride inside another milestone: a `core` refactor buried in a feature arc makes that arc's registry delta unreadable (the 6.1k `dialog`-footer lesson). **Not gated by anything, and it gates nothing** — it slots into any gap, and it should be taken **before** a fifth consumer appears, not after. **⚠️ 5th-instance trigger RECORDED (J-536): the M-RP-SETTINGS Leg-A Settings-sidebar rover IS the fifth — the fifth consumer has now appeared. Joe DEFERRED extraction (2026-07-17) rather than take it as a mid-arc rider on Leg B (which adds no new rover); the extraction is now OWED, to be taken before a sixth.**

> 🟡 **M-RP-PLUGIN-INSTALL — the "+" install affordance + the plugins pane action-bar (PENDING; filed J-538; slots after Leg C, gates nothing).** The Plugins pane gains a **static header strip** — a search field + action buttons (info / filter / `+`) — above the scrolling list. **NOT the grid `shelf`** (that is a grid-tailored `role="toolbar"` of command icon-faces with favourites/system + empty-collapse semantics; a search `textfield` is not a face — N-135): the strip is **composed shell-local** from `textfield` + `icon`-buttons, and the static-header / scroll-body layout is already the Leg-B settings-content scroll model (a skin, not a component). The **`+`** opens an install dialog over `AVAILABLE_CUSTOM` (D-119's `install()` path — the write-side the read-only list lacked). Legs, visible-first: **A** the action-bar (search + `+`) · **B** `+` → install dialog · **C** file the `pane-toolbar` candidate + CDP verify. Zero Rust. **`pane-toolbar` filed as a D-069 extraction candidate** (built at the 4th sighting: members / rooms / spaces headers, not at N=1). **Open for kickoff:** search = local filter of the visible list vs. catalogue search to install from. → JOURNAL J-538 · N-135.

> **✅ M-RP6.6 — CLIENT RESIDENT: live connection + traffic accounting — CLOSED (J-543). Single-seat (Chat technical lane; Ms Design owns the §7 row-swap). All three legs live-verified on real client 9222 + node: Leg A real lifecycle (connect→auth→READY, kill→DISCONNECTED — the emit bug found + fixed live, N-137), Leg B reconnect/backoff (schedule confirmed against wall-clock; auto-recovery no-restart), Leg C `CountingStream` byte accounting + RTT + `get_conn_stats` + `selfState.traffic` (live-cumulative bytes, real RTT). §0 ruled Path A (stream-layer counter). `cargo test` 1524/0/62 = floor 1519 + 5 new resident tests EXACTLY; npm 77; vite clean. D3 (GPL core untouched) + D2 (live ingest out) confirmed against the diff — 7 files, zero xgen-core. `tasks/M_RP6_6_IMPL.md` COMPLETED. Closes the live half of gate F-1. Owed: the ConnStats row-swap (Ms Design, §7); live ingest → own milestone (R5 + M-RP6.3). NEXT = M-RP6.3.** _(Phase-0 J-542; D1–D5 by-recomm, `tasks/M_RP6_6_RESIDENT.md`. PRECEDED M-RP6.3 per D5.)_ **The Tauri client has no resident, and nothing in the UI arc can fix that.** Grounded at M-RP6.1g: `xgen-client/src/desktop.rs::run_startup` performs **one** 2-second `connect_async`, **drops the stream** (`Ok(Ok(_stream))`), emits `Ready`, and **never touches the socket again**. So the client's connection state is **relaunch-scoped**: stopping the node while the app runs changes **nothing on screen**. The M-RP6.0 gate's **F-1** finding (*“no CDP-drivable connect/send pre-UI”*) is therefore only **half** closed by 6.1g — which closed the **read shape** (a real read verb, a real store, two real views). **Scope:** a sustained WS to the home node + a reconnect/backoff loop + a **live** lifecycle feeding the existing `xgen-client-state-changed` emit (the channel already exists — **do not build a second one**, D-067). **This is a Rust/protocol arc, NOT a UI one.** The desktop shell's own doc-comment has said since M1 that *“the full long-lived client resident … is wired in 2b / M3”* — **it never was**, and that gap has been silently inherited by every UI milestone since. **Only when this lands is *“stop the node → the led flips”* a runnable proof.** Until then the honest proof is relaunch-scoped, and any milestone claiming otherwise is claiming a leg it cannot run.

> ### ✅ **M-RP6.3 Leg A — RESIDENT STATUS PUBLICATION — CLOSED (J-545). Single-seat (Chat technical lane, J-544 autonomy grant). Zero `xgen-core`, zero sampler, zero node — 4 files (`resident.rs`, `desktop.rs`, `self-state.svelte.ts`, `app_client.svelte`). Code + records ready; Joe pushes.** **The retry is now BOUNDED and the numbers behind it are PUBLISHED.** §0's G-1/G-2 said a `(2/10)` counter and a countdown were **not expressible** — both now are. Shipped: **`RECONNECT_MAX_ATTEMPTS = 10`** (grounded: `1·2·4·8·16·30×5` = **181 s** of sleeping + up to 10 × `CONNECT_TIMEOUT` ⇒ ≈6 min to terminal — rides out a node restart, still parks a closed-lid laptop) · `Backoff::attempt()`/`exhausted()` (pure, clock-free) · **`ResidentStatus`** shared atomics + **`get_resident_status`**, deliberately the shipped **`TrafficCounters`/`get_conn_stats` shape folded into the EXISTING 2 s poll — one pattern, no second push channel (D-067)** · a **`watch::Sender<u64>` resume channel, the `PipeShutdown` sibling**, so the parked resident `select!`s on it as it already does on shutdown (ONE loop, ONE owner of `Reconnecting`) · `selfState.resident` verbatim snake_case · the `resident.reconnect` command + `focus`/`online` auto-resume. **⚠️ A DEFECT CAUGHT IN CHAT'S OWN EDIT BEFORE IT RAN:** `#[derive(Default)]` on `ResidentStatus` seeds the deadline to **`0` — a real 1970 epoch-ms** — which would have published a **PHANTOM COUNTDOWN from boot** on a connected client; hand-written `Default` + a test that fails if anyone re-derives it. *`TrafficCounters` needed the same hand-written `Default` for `RTT_NONE`: the second occurrence is what proves absent-not-zero is a rule, not a preference.* **VERIFIED LIVE 9222 + real node (Rule 5, every leg re-driven):** V1 quiescent `attempt:0 · max_attempts:10 · next_attempt_in_ms:null · terminal:false` + both window constants **arriving as DATA** (D5 proven on the wire) · **V2 the guard** — resume on a LIVE resident returns **`false`**, so a focus storm cannot reset a live backoff · **V3 the numbers** — T+6 s `2/10` countdown **113 ms** while the state is already `CONNECTING` (**the G-4 handoff caught mid-act**), T+16 s `4/10` countdown **6238 ms** · **V4 the cap** — `10/10 · terminal:true · countdown null`, **and the stronger proof: the node was brought BACK while parked and the client did NOT reconnect**, so the cap genuinely STOPS the resident rather than flagging it · **V5 the re-arm** — resume → `true` → real auth → **READY**, `attempt` 0, traffic live. **Gates: `cargo test` 1530/0/62 = floor 1524 + EXACTLY the 6 new tests · vite 183 · npm 77 · sampler 328.** **🔑 THE FINDING — AN UNATTRIBUTABLE STATE CHANGE, AND THE REFUSAL TO GUESS AT IT.** During the first park the resident left the terminal state on its own; the obvious story was *"the harness focused the window"* and **that story is FALSE** — CDP-installed counters read **`focus:0, online:0`**. **The cause is still unknown.** Rather than write an inferred diagnosis into the record (**the N-118/N-124 shape that has already burned this arc twice**), the hole was closed: `resume_resident` now takes a **`source`** and logs it — re-driven, the log reads `resume accepted source=dev-bridge`. *The event stays unexplained AND recorded as unexplained; it can never recur unexplained.* **⚠️ Consequence Joe should see: any `focus` resumes a parked resident, so `terminal` is durable only while the app is IGNORED** — the sleeping-laptop case D4 was written for, **not** a general "stop trying" guarantee; **Leg C must not draw it as permanent.** **The `[Reconnect]` VERB is live; its ELEMENT is deliberately ABSENT** (appearance → Ms Design, §5) — the `layout.revert` precedent, the opposite of painted-dead chrome (J-500/6.1j). **N-117 fired** (first `cargo test` returned `0/0/0` — the dev client held the exe; > **🟢 M-RP6.3 — LIVE MESSAGING: R6 COMPOSER + NARROW-B SEND PATH — PHASE-0 LOCKED (J-544). Design-only, no code; `tasks/M_RP6_3_COMPOSER.md` v1.0 ACTIVE. D1–D6 arc-local, by-recomm under Joe's autonomy grant (*"after we assert and agreed the form of socket, you have autonomy — except visual appearance"*).** **The send path is B (unify): outbound multiplexes over the resident's ONE socket.** Chosen on a USER argument — and grounding **narrowed** it. **Under A most of M-RP6.6 would be DECORATIVE**: a client that only opens a socket to send has no sustained thing to be in a *state* about, so the led, the lifecycle, RECONNECTING and the traffic rows would all describe a fiction (D-065, not performance). Plus **ordering** (one socket = one ordered stream) and **offline send**. *The honest half: the user never experiences architecture until it FAILS — B adds a silent failure mode A lacked.* **🔑 SIX GROUNDING FINDINGS @ `aa3cc3e`:** (1) **no retry cap exists** (`run_resident` loops forever; `Backoff{attempt}` private, getter-less, saturating) → **`(2/10)` is not expressible today**; (2) **`next_attempt_at` is not published** → **a countdown is not expressible today**; (3) the lifecycle sink is a **bare enum, no payload** → a 2nd published surface mirroring `TrafficCounters`/`get_conn_stats` (one pattern, not a new mechanism, D-067); (4) **the detection window is ~10 s twice over** (`PING_INTERVAL` + `CONNECT_TIMEOUT`) → **the countdown MUST hand off to `connecting…`** or zero-then-silence reads as frozen; (5) **⚠️ `send_event_confirmed` owns the connection and EATS the drain** (`connection.rs:186`, `_ => continue` at `:243`) — fan-out is swallowed while a send awaits its ack, and it is a **family** (`upload_blob`, `fetch_blob`, `get_dag_tips`), sweep **~114 client call sites**; (6) **no auth tiers, no session lifetime** (`CHALLENGE_EXPIRY_SECS=30` bounds the CHALLENGE; `ws://` only). **🔒 D1 — NARROW B: the resident socket is the CONTROL/MESSAGE PLANE ONLY** (Joe-confirmed, the one call not taken under the grant). `ops::*` keep their own short-lived connections — already correct under **D-056**. *B was decided for the user's messaging experience; that is the composer, not `create_space`.* **🔒 D2 — THREE PLANES, a STANDING INVARIANT:** control/message → the resident socket · bulk → own connection per transfer · real-time media → out of scope. **BINDING ON ALL FUTURE MILESTONES: the own-connection-and-drain family may NEVER run on the resident socket** — a blob upload there blinds the client to fan-out for the whole transfer. **🔒 D3** non-blocking drain is a constraint, not a style; `ops::send` stays a **software** fallback, never a **network** one. **🔒 D4** bounded retry + terminal `disconnected — [Reconnect]` (a real action, not a greyed spinner) + auto-resume on focus/network-return — both halves required. **🔒 D5** shape settable, do NOT wire settable (named constants, values published to the UI, **no config-plumbing layer** — migration waits on **J-513**). **🔒 D6** fail-fast default: **block send, never block typing**; a long outage OFFERS "send when reconnected", never a silent queue. **BINDING: a message may never LOOK sent when it is not** — and a half-open socket means the first send after a drop can fail EVEN WITH THE GUARD IN PLACE. **LEGS: A** resident status publication · **B** outbound multiplexer + ack correlation in the drain (**live ingest — the M-RP6.6 deferral — wires here**) · **C** R5 wrap + the **live mutable item class R5 does not have today** (counts, hands off to `connecting…`, **collapses in place** to `connection restored · 8s`; ~2 s grace — *silence is the correct UI for a blip*) · **D** R6 composer — **a BUILD, not a wrap**. **ALL APPEARANCE IN LEGS C+D → Ms Design** (§5 specifies what states exist, what they mean, when they appear — no colour, type, spacing, glyph or animation). → JOURNAL J-544.

Leg B only read counts ⇒ latent; **C2 is the FIRST reader of that field**, and keying the allowlist on `event_type` would have matched nothing — an empty stream reading as a dead projection. ***The phantom-defect family a THIRD time on this arc.*** **FILED: fix at source.** **🔑 THE RE-DRIVE UPGRADED V7 FROM SYNTHETIC TO MEASURED.** The handback's leg table showed C-3 satisfied while its own deviation list said V7/V8/V13 used synthetic `ingest.push` — **the summary line outranked the deviation line**, on the one binding set in capitals. Chat drove it: `bob` left+rejoined a ROOM → live `membership.leave`+`join`, **`room_id` len 83**, rendered; then left+rejoined the SPACE → `received:4`, two with **`room_id === ""`**, **`projectedCount:2`**, rows unchanged. **The Space pair is correctly excluded, MEASURED on the live wire.** *Zero was correct at J-546/J-547; here it is correct for a reason that was SEEN.* Verb confirmed from `join --help`, never fabricated. **V11 — REAL OUTAGE THROUGH THE PRODUCTION PATH:** node killed abruptly, episode published via the shell `$effect`→`gaps.apply` seam, `dialling`→`counting-down`→`resolved`, `resolvedAfterMs:71183`, **`probeSurvived:true` on Chat's own stamp**, `nodeCount:1`. Clair 103218 vs Chat 71183 — **different and both correct, each measuring its own outage**; *a number that SHOULD differ between seats is not a failure to reproduce.* **⚠️ ONE GATE DID NOT REPRODUCE: CLIENT VITE IS 192, NOT 191** (measured twice; the only intervening commit is CSS-rules-only and cannot move a module graph — *reasoned, not separately measured*, N-108). **C2 added +8 to the 184 floor, not +7 — LEG D'S FLOOR IS 192**, and a floor quoted one low reads as a phantom regression next leg. npm **114** and sampler **169** reproduced exactly. **⚠️ ONE FILED OBSERVATION DID NOT SURVIVE BEING DRIVEN:** *fold→unfold resets the room latch* — **it does not**; the latch effect re-reads `selection.current` on mount and re-latches. **Real but strictly NARROWER than filed.** **Invariant that fell out: the latch cannot change unless the BUS changed.** **🔑 F-5 STOPPED BEING THEORETICAL:** the 520 events pushed to force `dropped > 0` **evicted the room's REAL messages through the GLOBAL `INGEST_CAP`** — `projectedCount` fell to **0** at the instant the head marker flipped to *“… some were dropped”*. **The defect and the confession it exists for, in one frame** — the clearest vindication yet of C-6, and a stronger case for M-RP6.4 than the filed note. **ALSO REPRODUCED:** V1 **134** twice after full reloads, state stated · V2 tile **“Messages”**, `placeholderIds:[]`, `plugin-list` row — **one descriptor, three readers, ZERO register lines** (F-1) · **V3, the leg C1 could not drive** — fill against a **region leaf** at two tile sizes with a real splitter moved between reads, **`fillDelta` constant 16**, `docScrollable:false` both (*the CONSTANT is the proof, not either absolute*) · V4 — **the measurement trap confirmed live: `count` reads 2 on an EMPTY room** · V5 real send painted, `isOwn:false`, initials-only author · V6 filtered-not-dropped + the switch-half · V8 marker **mutated in place**, `__head__` unchanged · V9 **one synchronous expression**, all **0** · V10 0 in grace → exactly 1 after · **V12 widget genuinely UNMOUNTED and the episode survived intact — the cleanest justification for C-10** · V13 driven **against a control**, `groupedCount 1 → 0`. **⚠️ SCOPE NOTE:** after a real unmount the probe is gone — **element identity is claimed WITHIN a mount, never across one**; episode identity survives, DOM identity does not. **DEVIATIONS, all six accepted** (6 files not 4 for the pure `derive.ts` + 21 tests · shell `$effect` feeder · always-mount empty rows · synthetic V7/V8/V13, since re-driven · structural `<style>` · functional copy). **OPEN, UNEXPLAINED, INSTRUMENTED:** the latch moved with no click during membership churn — **not attributed, not guessed at** (J-545); per the invariant, whatever moved it moved the BUS. **TEST ENV CHANGED:** `LegBSpace` now holds `legc2redrive`; `bob`'s membership restored; session carries `dropped:26`. **No new D, no new `core`, no new N.** **FILED:** `event_type`→`type` · **M-RP6.8 view-latch persistence** · message-selection = a `core` union change · N-121 dedup · per-message send state → Leg D. **🟢 NEXT-ACTIVE = M-RP6.3 Leg D — R6 composer + send. R6 DOES NOT EXIST: A BUILD, NOT A WRAP**, and it owns the per-message send-status indicator. **⚠️ LEG D FLOORS: cargo 1541/0/62 · npm 114 · vite 192 client / 169 sampler · sampler 386 · registry 134 quiescent.** → JOURNAL J-550 · `tasks/M_RP6_3_LEG_C2_STREAM_PANEL.md` v1.1 COMPLETED §8 · `tasks/M_RP6_3_COMPOSER.md` v1.6.

> ### 🟢 **M-RP6.3 Leg D — R6 COMPOSER + SEND PATH — PHASE-0 LOCKED (J-552). Design + records only, NO CODE. `tasks/M_RP6_3_COMPOSER.md` v1.8 §9.11. Gates untouched.** **⚠️ THE FIRST PHASE-0 ON THIS ARC THAT DID NOT COME BACK SHELL-ONLY — LEG D TOUCHES `.rs` AND `core`, AND A MILESTONE IS INSERTED AHEAD OF IT.** **Two corrections to the brief, both material:** the four-way is `accepted`·**`rejected`**·`timed_out`·`failed` (`resident.rs:706`) — *in-flight is not an outcome, it is the state before one exists*, and `rejected` is a deterministic refusal carrying a wire `code`, **a message that will never arrive**; and **`event_id` arrives WITH the outcome**, so the brief's "pending store keyed by `event_id`" **is not constructible** — keyed by a client-minted local id, real id stitched on at outcome. **🔑 C-4 AMENDED IN PLACE:** the node excludes the author from fan-out **BY IDENTITY** (`fanout.rs:305`, EV-D2), so a send returns `accepted` and **the stream does not change** — the user's own words render nowhere. The brief's echo *"dropped when the real event arrives"* **cannot work: the real event NEVER arrives.** LOCKED — C-4 governs **inbound** unchanged; **outbound is a NAMED exception**, a session-mortal never-federated echo store in `$common`. **Twelve user-facing locks** incl. **three visual states not two** (sent · **unresolved** · not sent — *collapsing `timed_out` either way is the D6 lie verbatim*) · **retry by status** (`failed` free · `rejected` never · `timed_out` only behind a **double-post warning**) · the C-6 head marker **must cover the user's OWN sends** · **N windows** not two · no room latched ⇒ **type yes, send no**. **⚠️ THE SILENT QUEUE D6 FORBIDS IS ALREADY SHIPPED:** `send_message` never checks link state and `outbound_rx.recv()` is polled **only inside `run_session`'s `select!`** — between sessions **nothing drains the queue**, so the promise **never resolves**, the message sits, and the drain writes it **minutes later, unannounced**. `SEND_ACK_TIMEOUT` (10 s) ages only sends already WRITTEN; **a queued-but-never-written send has no timer at all.** *Nobody was careless — it is the seam between two correct pieces, reachable only once a composer exists.* **LOCKED (D1): bound the wait, return `failed` NOT `timed_out`** (*`failed` = never reached the wire, which is exactly true; `timed_out` would claim the node may hold it — the D6 lie inverted*); the frontend guard is the nicety, **the timeout is the guarantee** (§0 G-4). **🔑 A USER QUESTION ABOUT EMOJI RETIRED CHAT'S OWN RECOMMENDATION.** `details` is live — but **grouped continuation rows suppress the whole header line, name AND `details`**, so three sends in a row means **a `failed` third message looks exactly like the delivered first**. Chat recommended **perforating grouping**; Joe asked *"what if I want to add an emoji to one of the grouped messages?"* and grounding that answered the send-status question instead: **`bodyExtras` sits BELOW the body, outside the `{#if !grouped}` block — grouping-immune by position**, and `types.ts:69` has named it for *attachments / reactions* all along. **Suppression is CORRECT** (repeating name+time on every row of a run is what grouping exists to remove) — ***punching a hole in a right rule to admit one tenant is the wrong repair; the tenant was in the wrong socket.*** **LOCKED: send-status → `bodyExtras`, and the socket gets BUILT** (declared, never rendered ⇒ a `core` change — but **building a socket that was always designed**); `types.ts:67–69` corrected, because that line was written **before a composer existed, when nobody had asked whether send-status is header chrome or per-row state.** **NO provisional-timestamp field** — `SendOutcome` carries **no timestamp**, so even on `accepted` there is nothing to correct the time TO; *a provisional marker that can never resolve is worse than none.* **🔑 JOE INSERTS A MILESTONE:** build `bodyExtras` **fixture-driven first** (the R5/M-RP5.6 precedent) — ***a socket designed against a single static led will hold a single static led***; reactions are the far richer consumer (N mounts/row, runtime add/remove, interactive, on continuation rows). **What the build teaches that a walk cannot:** `cid()` under **N mounts × M rows** — registry **134 quiescent** underwrites every verify leg on this arc, and three mounts on twenty rows is sixty new ids **or sixty collisions**, unknown which. **⚠️ THE FENCE, BINDING:** the container renders `WidgetMount[]` and **never learns what a tag or reaction IS**; **fixtures live in the SAMPLER only** (in the client it renders **nothing** until fed — N-091/D-065); assets **locally bundled, never remote URLs** (D-111); **no `ReactionDescriptor`, no wire shape, no attribution** — *a data shape invented before the protocol exists is a shape the protocol then has to satisfy*, and **who reacted is identity data, on the no-anonymity core.** **⚠️ #11b MULTI-DEVICE — NAMED, NOT SOLVED:** exclusion by identity + fan-out to **every connection** of a recipient ⇒ other people's messages reach all their devices **and yours reach none of yours**; type on the laptop and **the sentence does not exist on the phone**. **The node DID persist it** — fan-out is live delivery, **not the record** — so **the hole closes at M-RP6.4**, under a constraint written now while free: **backfill reads the EVENT STORE and must return the requesting identity's OWN events.** **LEG ORDER: 🟢 D1 send-path honesty (Rust, FIRST — a LIVE D6 violation)** · **M-RP6.9 `bodyExtras` container (`core`, fixture-driven)** · **D2 composer + echo store (`$common`)** · **D3 send-status widget (`$common`)**. **Floors: cargo 1541/0/62 (D1 moves it BY DESIGN) · npm 114 · vite 192 client / 169 sampler · sampler 386 · registry 134 quiescent.** **FILED: M-RP-REACTIONS** (blob-backed custom reactions — custom sets, animated, >16px, not unicode; **discussion deliberately deferred to the real interface**, no design record) · no authoritative timestamp on `accepted` · the M-RP6.4 event-store constraint. **OWED unchanged:** ConnStats row-swap + **all Leg C/D appearance** → Ms Design, who should know **`bodyExtras` is a socket two future arcs will also mount into**. → JOURNAL J-552 · `tasks/M_RP6_3_COMPOSER.md` v1.8 §9.11.

> **🟡 M-RP6.9 — `bodyExtras`: THE PER-ROW MESSAGE CONTAINER (`core`, fixture-driven). PENDING, filed J-552 at Joe's call; inserted between Leg D1 and Leg D2.** `MessageDescriptor.bodyExtras` is **declared and never rendered** (`types.ts:81`, `message.svelte:29` — reserved-unfed, D-065). It sits **below the body, outside the `{#if !grouped}` block**, so it is the **only per-row socket that survives grouping** — which is why **both** future tenants want it: send-status (Leg D3) and reactions. **Built against the STRONG consumer, deliberately:** N mounts per row, runtime add/remove, interactive, on continuation rows. **The R5 precedent exactly** — M-RP5.6 built the stream on fixtures and Leg C wrapped it live four milestones later. **⚠️ THE FENCE IS THE MILESTONE:** the container renders `WidgetMount[]` and **never learns what its contents MEAN**; the sampler passes fixture mounts, a later arc passes real ones, and **the component does not change between those two events** — *that is what makes it a container to complete rather than a stub to replace*. **Sampler-only fixtures** (the real client renders **nothing** until fed — a client showing invented tags is fake data on screen); **locally bundled assets, never remote URLs** (D-111 must not be pre-figured even by a fixture); **ZERO protocol, ZERO store, ZERO federation, no `ReactionDescriptor`.** **The real question it answers: `cid()` behaviour under N mounts × M rows** — sixty new registry ids or sixty collisions, and **which is not known**; answer it on a fixture bench, not during a composer drive where it would look like the composer's fault. Sampler catalogue moving off **386** is the honest signal it shipped. → JOURNAL J-552 · COMPOSER §9.11.6.

> **🟡 M-RP-REACTIONS — BLOB-BACKED CUSTOM REACTIONS. PENDING, filed J-552; unscoped, no design record.** Joe's vision, recorded so it is not re-derived: **not unicode characters** — customizable sets of visual tags, **animated**, at resolutions **well above ~16px**, in the Discord/Skype shape. **The word "reaction" is taken deliberately** (generic UI vocabulary; it is what users already know). **Discussion is DEFERRED to the milestone that builds the real interface** — mentioned here only to fix **the purpose of the M-RP6.9 container**, which is why that container is built against it rather than against a single static led. **Nothing is designed here**, and three consequences are recorded rather than resolved: it is **blob-backed** (content-addressed — the M12 store, not a `string` emoji field) · it lands on **R-5 / M-BLOB-PLANE** · and **custom sets sit squarely on D-110/D-111** (a Space supplying assets that render in your client is the shape D-110 refused — the answer is cheap **if decided early**: content-addressed, fetched from your OWN node, **never a host a Space chooses**). **Who reacted is identity data — Joe's, on the no-anonymity core.**

> **🟡 M-RP6.8 — VIEW-LATCH PERSISTENCE ACROSS REMOUNT + THE UNATTRIBUTED BUS MOVE. PENDING, filed J-550; frontend, small, gates nothing.** Two related observations from the C2 re-drive. **(a)** A region widget's view latch is widget-`$state` (the `rooms-panel` D3 precedent), so it is rebuilt on remount from `selection.current`. **That recovers correctly whenever the bus still holds the right kind** — Chat drove fold→unfold and the room latch SURVIVED, which **disproves the broader form of the handback's filed note**. The residue is narrow: the latch resets only when the bus no longer holds a matching entity at remount time. **(b) ⚠️ Unattributed:** during the C-3 drive the stream's latch moved rooms **with no click**, amid `bob`'s membership churn. **Recorded, not guessed at** (J-545). **The invariant that scopes the search: the latch effect writes ONLY when `kind === 'room'`, so the latch cannot change unless the SELECTION BUS changed** — so this is a bus-writer question, not a widget question. *Filing an arc is not deciding to build it.*

> **🟡 M-SEC-TLS — `wss://` TRANSPORT + CERTIFICATE HANDLING. PENDING, filed J-544; Rust/protocol.** `resident.rs::ws_authority` documents Phase 1 as **`ws://` only — no TLS**, so every byte the resident sends is plaintext. MLS/E2E protects message **content**; it does not protect **metadata** — who talks to whom, when, how often, how much (and M-RP6.6's Leg C now literally counts those bytes). **🔒 GATE: `wss://` is a HARD PREREQUISITE before the resident dials anything NON-LOOPBACK** — a gate, not a preference. **Does NOT block M-RP6.3**, which is loopback-scoped: *the gate is on DEPLOYMENT, not on the milestone.* Sequenced FIRST of the three security arcs because it is the cheapest and unblocks the most. **Carries a known follow-on:** M-RP6.6's `CountingStream` sits below the WS handshake on a raw `TcpStream`; counting over TLS is a documented open question in `resident.rs`.

> **🟡 M-SEC-AUTHSESS — SESSION LIFETIME, RE-AUTH, AND AUTH SCOPES. PENDING, filed J-544; Rust/protocol — AND ⚠️ NOT A TECHNICAL SCOPING CALL.** Grounded at J-544: `xgen-core/src/transport/auth.rs` is nonce → Ed25519 → verify, and **that is all**. `CHALLENGE_EXPIRY_SECS = 30` bounds the **challenge**, not the session. There is **no session expiry, no re-auth, no scopes, no roles, no privilege levels** anywhere in the transport auth layer — authentication is **binary and permanent for the life of the socket**. Three consequences the unified socket sharpened: **(a)** B does not create this weakness but **extends its duration by orders of magnitude** — under a per-message connection a stolen socket is worth seconds; under a resident it is worth as long as the app runs; **(b)** M6's **node-admin write-path verbs** would, on a shared socket, inherit **byte-identical** authentication to sending a chat message — today admin/user separation is held **BY CONVENTION (D1/D-056), NOT BY MECHANISM**, and that is recorded honestly rather than papered over; **(c)** no re-auth means a long-lived session never re-proves key possession. **🔒 JOE LOCKS THIS ONE.** *"What is an identity authorized to do, and for how long"* sits directly on the **no-anonymity core** — in an identity-verified protocol this is not a bolt-on, and it may need spec/doc work before any code. **Chat explicitly DECLINED to lock it under the J-544 autonomy grant.** Flagged as possibly large enough to deserve **its own grounding session before it is even scoped**. Sequenced BEFORE M-BLOB-PLANE so the blob plane inherits a settled session model rather than being retrofitted twice.

> **🟡 M-BLOB-PLANE — BULK TRANSFER CONNECTIONS: CANCEL, RESUME, PROGRESS, BINARY FRAMING. PENDING, filed J-544; Rust/protocol.** The **bulk plane** of D2's three-plane split. Grounded at J-544: `Connection::upload_blob` (`connection.rs:256`) / `fetch_blob` (`:323`) chunk **base64 (+33% overhead)** over the WS **and own the connection and drain until their reply** — so on the resident socket a large transfer would leave the client **blind to inbound fan-out for the entire duration** (minutes on a big file), with **no cancel, no resume, no progress**, and multiple files serialising. **🔒 Hence D2's binding invariant: blobs get their OWN connection per transfer** — parallel-capable, independently cancellable, and free to starve without touching messaging. WebSocket has **no multiplexing** (unlike HTTP/2 or QUIC), so head-of-line blocking on one socket is inherent, not a bug to design away; `BLOB_CHUNK_BYTES` chunking is the mitigation on the message plane and is not sufficient for the bulk one. Sequenced LAST of the four so it inherits both TLS and a settled session model.

> **🟡 M-RP-ICON-ADOPT — glyph consolidation (PENDING — **MODEL LOCKED**, D-108/D-109, J-504):** **⚠️ v0.1's premise was WRONG and is corrected: the glyphs were never *"inline `<svg>` in the field components"* — grepped, there are ZERO.** Measured (2026-07-12): **21 distinct glyphs across FOUR mechanisms** — 3 `<path d>` in `icons.ts`; **10 `mask-image` + 7 `background-image` data-URIs in `skin.css`**; 1 `.svg` file (**re-inlined a second time** in the sampler). **And every skin glyph token was declared inside its own component's selector, none at `:root` — so `--tri`/`--tri-open` are ALREADY DECLARED TWICE** (`.combobox` + `.section`, whose comment claims it *"reuses"* them). **Locked model (D-108):** a glyph is a **SKIN TOKEN** — **`core` owns the NAME, the skin owns the SHAPE**; `.svg` + a licence manifest → codegen → `glyphs.generated.css` (`:root { --glyph-* }`, **the runtime default**, L1.5 below `skin.css`) + `icons.generated.ts` (`type IconName`, **names only**). **CDP-proven on the real client** (§5 of the canonical doc): CSS **`d:`** works in WebView2, **`d: var(--token)` resolves**, **`var()` resolves inside a custom-property value** → **ONE** skin rule serves every icon; a later stylesheet **redraws any glyph** with **zero component change**. **Three of the five v0.1 questions DISSOLVED** — multi-colour stays an `icon` (**D-096 not re-opened**), stroke-vs-fill is a skin property (**`icon` gains no prop**), the combo triangle folds. Canonical: **`ui/docs/xgen-css-layer-model.md` v1.0** · `docs/xgen-icon-adoption.md` **v1.0** · N-101. **Phase-0 is now classification + provenance, not re-litigation.** Parked behind the frame arc; **gate unchanged — it does not jump the queue.**

> **✅ CSS LAYER MODEL + THE SPACE-THEME GLYPH BAN — canonical doc created, BOTH DRIFTS CLOSED (J-504 → J-505).** `ui/docs/xgen-css-layer-model.md` **v1.1** owns the shipped appearance stack (the layout sibling of `xgen-region-dock-model.md`): **`theme-*.css` (custom skin, override layer) → `skin.css` + `glyphs.generated.css` (ONE layer, the default skin — split by WHO WRITES IT, hand vs generated) → the normalizes (reset, not skin).** **A theme overrides a glyph exactly the way it overrides a colour — the cascade IS the mechanism.** **✅ DRIFT 1 CLOSED — Ch6 §6.2 REWRITTEN (Ch6 v0.5, Session 10):** `tokens.css` never existed · `skin-dark.css` → `skin.css` · the glyph bank added as L1.5 · and **the reversal that matters — component `<style>` blocks are FORBIDDEN, not required** (N-025/N-031/N-090). **D-057/D-058 superseded in PART, not deleted** (intent survives; file structure does not). *The principle, because it reads backwards until you see it: **the rule that makes skinning TOTAL is the rule that forbids the component from participating in it.*** **✅ DRIFT 2 CLOSED — D-110, and it is a TRUST boundary, not a styling one:** **A SPACE MAY RE-COLOUR. A SPACE MAY NOT RE-DRAW, AND MAY NOT RE-LAYOUT.** Ch6 §6.3's Layer 3 is a theme **declared by a Space OWNER and delivered over the wire**, so an unrestricted Layer 3 would let a Space owner **redraw a lock, a warning, a verified mark, or the AI badge (§6.13)** — icon spoofing in an identity-verified protocol. Colour (incl. the glyph **tint**) ✅; **geometry (`--glyph-*`, `--glyph-*-url`) ❌**; layout/metrics ❌; **everything not on the allowlist ❌ by default**. Specified in **new Ch6 §6.3.1 / §6.3.2** — which answers a question Ch6 had carried open since **Session 1**. **🔑 Two consequences that are NORMATIVE, not cosmetic:** **(a) a key allowlist alone is THEATRE** — a Space theme is a key→value **map**, but a client that string-concatenates a stylesheet lets a malicious **VALUE** escape its declaration and inject arbitrary CSS; **mandatory: `element.style.setProperty()` (CSSOM cannot break out) + `CSS.supports()` value validation + Space-subtree scoping — all three, any one alone is insufficient**; **(b) the ban BINDS THE GENERATOR BACK** — **`--glyph-*-url` MUST be emitted COLOUR-FREE**, because a data-URI with colour baked in **fuses colour and geometry into one token**, making the ban unenforceable on exactly those glyphs → **the re-emit of the seven `%23e6e6e6` glyphs is now a SECURITY REQUIREMENT, not a Phase-0 tidy-up.** **🔑 Locked BEFORE implementation — grepped: `state.space_theme` appears in NO Rust, NO TS, NO Svelte.** The theming cascade is **specified and entirely unbuilt**; **no milestone may claim theming works, and none may ship a Layer-3 applier that does not implement Ch6 §6.3.2 in full.** **Still open (flagged, not solved):** the exact colour-token allowlist · user-disable of Space themes.

> **🔒 D-111 — A CLIENT MUST NOT FETCH A HOST CHOSEN BY SOMEONE ELSE; link previews are NODE-SIDE (J-506, Ch2 v1.3).** **⚠️ Opens with a RETRACTION:** J-504/J-505 listed *"`url()` fetches"* among the open Space-owner trust surfaces. **It is not one** — under **D-110's own** colour-only allowlist, `CSS.supports('color', 'url(…)')` is **false**; a `url()` **cannot enter a Space theme at all**. Fonts likewise are **bundled in the binary** (Ch6 §6.2). **A threat named from pattern-matching, in the same document that forecloses it — retracted, not hedged.** **🔑 But the grep that killed it found the real invariant, and found the protocol had ALREADY WON IT:** *any mechanism where the client fetches a URL chosen by **someone else** turns the client into a **beacon** — leaking the reader's **IP** and **read-time** to a host of the **sender's** choosing. XGen publishes your XGID by design; it does **NOT** publish your network location.* **And every avenue is already shut, structurally: `message.image`/`message.file` carry `xgen://hash/sha256:<64-hex>` — a CONTENT ADDRESS, NOT A LOCATION** (`blob_store.rs`; federation-native M12/J-389; per-blob client-encrypted; store *"content-blind by construction"*), **no HTTP client in any crate**, fonts bundled, themes URL-free. **🔑 A HASH CANNOT NAME A HOST — the beacon isn't *blocked*, it is UNSAYABLE.** *Content-addressing was chosen for integrity and bought privacy for free; nobody had written that down.* **⚠️ THE ONE ROW THAT INVITES IT BACK: `link previews`, in Ch2's "Client decisions — implementation freedom" table.** Unbuilt, but a client fetching preview URLs from message text hands the **sender** every reader's IP and read-time, invisibly, on every message. **✅ D-111: link previews — and any rendering that resolves an outbound URL — are fetched NODE-SIDE, never client-side.** The Node already talks to the world; the Client deliberately does not. One fetch per link, not one per reader. **Freedom preserved** — *whether* and *how* to show previews stay the client's; **only the fetch LOCATION is fixed, because it is not a rendering decision — it is a privacy boundary wearing a rendering decision's clothes.** **⚠️ WHAT ACTUALLY REMAINS, and it dwarfs the rest: D-036 MODULE WIDGETS** — third-party HTML in an isolated webview; **CSP/sandboxing still OPEN at Ch6 §6.8.8** (filed Session 2, untouched since). **Unlike blobs, themes and glyphs, it has NO structural foreclosure at all.** Ch6's, not the UI track's. **Filed, not solved.**

> **🟢 M-RP7 — THE DOCK ENGINE ARC (renderer B). Numbering per `docs/xgen-dock-engine-phase0.md` §11, amended at J-515.** ✅ **M-RP7.1 — the tile frame: stripe, grip, fold** (CLOSED J-514) · ✅ **M-RP7.1b — the fold axis becomes the user's choice; splits shrink-wrap; the hole gets a floor** (CLOSED J-516) · ✅ **M-RP7.2 — splitter resize on the seam** (CLOSED J-519, `9faa38c`) · 🟢 **M-RP7.3 — the mutation algebra (pure)** (**🔒 OPENS BY FIXING N-120** — `path` is threaded over the RESOLVED tree while `resizeSplit` walks the DESCRIPTOR, and `resolve.ts` drops → **a resize can invert**; a required leg, not a filed item. Then a module beside `resolve.ts`: `move` · `fold` · `resize`; remove → collapse-degenerate → insert → re-normalise. **Vitest, no DOM, no gestures**) · 🟡 **M-RP7.4 — drag to dock: grip, edge bands** (four edge bands per tile, **inert centre**; **⚠️ a HOLE IS NOT A DROP TARGET — a hole has no address**; where the arc's real cost lives) · 🟡 **M-RP7.5 — the session layout feeder — ✅ CLOSED (J-528)** (the grid finally **writes `session.layout`** — **⚠️ and then BOTH writers touch `session`: N-107 one level deeper, the merge must be PER-KEY INSIDE `session`, never a whole-object write, or it eats the `geometry` Rust just saved**) · 🟡 **M-RP7.6 — the grid lock: freeze arrangement, keep function — ✅ CLOSED (J-529, `303faa4`)** (a 4th bottom-shelf face; **it cannot ship earlier — today it would guard NOTHING**; it is the **first STATEFUL shelf face** — grepped: `shelf-face` has `active` (roving) and `disabled` (guard) and **no pressed/toggle concept**, so `aria-pressed` is a real `core` change; `locked` persists in `session` → **after M-RP7.5**; and *"lock the top shelf too"* **locks an empty box today** — the top shelf mounts `items={[]}` and collapses to height 0, so it joins the day favourites exist) · 🟡 **M-RP7.7 — node app inherits the frame + grid** (lands **after** the arc, so it inherits a **working** grid rather than building the frame twice). **FILED, NOT IN THIS ARC:** `M-RP-PLATE` (the grid backdrop — a background **widget**, not a skin rule; it **replaces** the dev raster; **and it CARRIES the region-owned gap model — that model needs transparent splits, and making the splits transparent IS this milestone**; gated on the settings mechanism) · `M-RP-RESTART` — File ▸ Restart and Revert (**lands with M-RP7.5**, which creates the state they discard) · `tabs` (D-116 — a position, not a deferral) · `M-RP-ROVING` · `M-RP-ENTITY-DRAG` (dragging **content**, not regions — **its hard part is that a DROP IS A PROTOCOL VERB**: dragging a person into a room is an *invite*; the M-RP6.6 shape — **a UI milestone cannot manufacture a verb that does not exist**) · `M-RP-ENTITY-PANEL-RESPONSIVE` (an entity list fills any rectangle; **the dock must never learn that `spaces` prefers to be tall** — container queries are SKIN, but **the real cost is 2-D roving, which is a DIFFERENT machine, not a fifth copy**) · **tear-off to a real OS window** (behind **M-RP8** — a torn-off region is a `decorations:false` `WebviewWindow` whose **title bar IS the tile stripe**, S-2's *one component, two mounts*; it also needs the descriptor to **record floating regions**, or W-13's re-inject silently re-docks them — **a second schema change, not free**) · `M-RP-SETTINGS` · `M-RP6.1m` · the `dialog` footer-snippet slot · the `mergeClasses` dedupe sweep.

> ⬛ **M-RP7 dock engine — SUPERSEDED 2026-07-12 by the dock-engine Phase-0 (§11). Original filing kept as history:** the owned Maya-style renderer B on the same layout descriptor. 7.1 layout tree + splitters · 7.2 drag + drop-zone overlays (hover-to-plug-in) · 7.3 save/restore layouts (auto-save-on-exit + auto-load-on-start; local `xgen-client_layout.json`, per-device, never federated) — **⚠️ DoD leg pinned at J-499/N-095: a missing / corrupt / schema-stale layout file MUST fall back to `DEFAULT_LAYOUT`, never to a blank centre, and the fallback must be EXERCISED (feed it a corrupt file), not asserted.** Measured at 6.1f: a null layout unmounts `region-shell` entirely — no crash, but no default either. Rust persists the tree as an **opaque blob** (the `get_substitutions` shape); the descriptor type is **never duplicated in Rust** (D-067, the J-499 D2 lock) · 7.4 custom-widget-contributed regions live · 7.5 (stretch) tear-off into separate OS windows (Tauri `WebviewWindow` + cross-window sync) · 7.6 named layouts + a layout-manager widget (save-as/rename/delete/set-active; `xgen-client_layouts.json`). Persistence spec: `xgen-region-dock-model.md` §9 (v1.1) — `widgetId` = durable identity, on-load reconcile drops unknown ids + re-injects missing `system` widgets (W-13).

> **⏸️ M-RP7.7 — node app inherits the frame + grid — POSTPONED until the client UI is complete (Joe, J-531; filed J-495; renumbered at J-514).** ⚠️ **GROUNDED J-531 — the “near-mechanical” claim below is WRONG:** the node has NO uistate store, NO `layout-default`, NO region-shell mount, NO plugin registry, and its `desktop.rs` exposes only `get_node_state` / `shut_down` (no `get/set_ui_state`, no geometry persistence). It sits at the **pre-J-495 stage**. The frame CHROME is core-parameterised, but the store + Rust verbs + node widget set must be **built**, not flipped — so 7.7 splits into **7.7a** (frame consolidation) / **7.7b** (Rust persistence) / **7.7c** (grid mount, placeholder region; the node region inventory is an unmade Phase-0 — §6.4 specs SCREENS, not dock regions). **D1 (a)** + **D2** (hide-to-tray IS the native window X, no new menu item) recorded as design intent; **D3/D4** deferred. *Original filing kept below as history:* the node app inherits the whole client frame (`menu-bar` + `status-bar` + centre region shell) rather than growing its own. Near-mechanical by construction — every piece is already `core` and shell-parameterised (`status-bar` has a `grip` prop, `menu-bar` takes a `menus` array, the keymap takes a command table, the accent is one CSS var); the node differs only in its **7 lifecycle states + degraded stacking** (its own `STATE_COLOURS`), its **blue accent**, and its **own command table**. **Sequenced after 6.1f deliberately** — let the client prove the region-shell scaffold first, then the node inherits frame + regions in one pass instead of building the frame twice. Also inherits the N-088 `#app{height:100%}` fix (or reproduces the bug).

> **✅ M-RP-PLATE — the grid backdrop plate — DONE (J-532, feat `6bd17c8`).** The dev raster is now a real element mounted through the plugin registry: `region-shell` gained a grid-wide background socket (`background?: WidgetMount[]` + `backgroundLive?` + a **separate `bgWidgets`** registry — the `message-stream` shape one level up), and `grid-plate` is the **first system plate widget** (`kind:'system'`, `delivery:'compiled'`, `surface:'none'` WITH a `component` — the first `surface:'none'` row that carries one; content the shell mounts into a **named host socket**, where `plugin-list` mounts into a dialog). **⚠️ Corrected from the promotion framing: the plate is `kind:'SYSTEM'`, not custom** — the promoted dev raster; it is the **mount/containment** exemplar, while **connection-stats** is the custom install→dock→uninstall exemplar. **🔒 Pointer lock (D-116) verified live 9222:** `pointer-events:none` — a fold-hole reveals the plate but `elementFromPoint` returns `region-split`, never the plate. **Inert half shipped; the live-switchable backdrop + its user setting ride `M-RP-SETTINGS`** (the J-513 settings gate is unchanged). NO Rust / schema / sampler / node; registry **69→73** (+4). Chat re-drove every leg (Rule 5) — incl. catching a stale-dev-client phantom count (N-132). The region-owned gap model was **NOT** carried here (that claim was retracted at J-521; it shipped alone at M-RP7.2b). **Next = connection-stats** (`surface:'region'`, `kind:'custom'`), then `M-RP-SETTINGS`. → JOURNAL J-532 · N-131 / N-132 · dock-engine §4.5.1 (BUILT) · D-118 / D-112 / D-103.

> **✅ M-RP-CONNSTATS — connection-stats: the custom-plugin lifecycle exemplar — DONE (J-533, commits `c747729` + `7f24b19`).** The first `kind:'custom'` widget, and the runtime **install→dock→uninstall** path it forced into being (→ **D-119**): a **reactive registry** (`widgetRegistry`/`bgWidgets`/titles = pure builders over a `$common` installed-set), an **available-vs-installed** split (`AVAILABLE_CUSTOM` ≠ `CLIENT_PLUGINS`), **register-before-`loadLayout`** boot-order (else W-13 drops the custom leaf), and **per-device persistence** (`session.installed`, N-107). Install = register + `insertLeaf`; uninstall = deregister + `removeRegion` (remove-without-blanking = the existing collapse-degenerate; D-085 intact, no code loader). Reads `selfState` (D-067, no new channel/Rust); **Speed/Bandwidth render honest `N/A`** reminder rows — an owner override of N-091 (data home = M-RP6.6). **Verified live 9222 after a full reload:** install **73→85 (+12)** enumerated · uninstall **85→73** exact, no blank · **persist across reload** (install→reload→survives→uninstall→reload→gone) · W-13 phantom leaf `droppedCount:1` · `cargo test` **1517/0/62 IDENTICAL** · npm **77** · vite **173**. NO Rust / schema / sampler. **Next = M-RP-SETTINGS** (reuses D-119's install path for the real install/uninstall UI + delivers the plate's backdrop-setting). → JOURNAL J-533 · D-119 · N-133 · components v0.78.

> **🟡 M-RP8 `title-bar` core + frameless restore (PENDING, filed J-495):** the deferred endpoint of the J-493 frameless design. The client currently ships **`decorations: true`** (native title bar) as a **temporary development affordance** — a frameless window during build has no window controls and no way to move it (Joe, 2026-07-11). **The honest finding that fixed the sequencing:** a *customised native* caption cannot deliver what is actually wanted — **extra buttons are impossible** and only **colours** are themeable (Win11 DWM attributes, silently ignored on Win10). The Discord-style bar people picture **IS** a custom (frameless) title bar; there is no cheap middle option, and an interim DWM colour-tint was considered and **rejected** as work we would delete. **Scope:** a `title-bar` `core` component (drag-region root + app title + minimise/maximise/close **seams**, the same shape as the proven `onResizeGrip`) + room for extra buttons + 4 new `icons.ts` glyphs + `decorations:false` in both apps + the `core:window:allow-start-dragging` grant back. **Scheduled AFTER the widget grid is live on BOTH apps** — by then the client and node frames are structurally identical, so it is **one component and two mounts** rather than building it twice. **Cheap by construction:** the `onResizeGrip → startResizeDragging` seam — the only genuinely risky part of going frameless — is **already built, granted and proven end-to-end** (J-495) and stays wired throughout, so it never has to be rebuilt. **Note for the builder:** the title-bar must own its **own** drag-region root — it cannot ride the menu-bar's strip (the `core` skin sets `.menu-bar{width:100%}`, and Tauri drags only when the **event target itself** carries `data-tauri-drag-region`, never an ancestor — learned the hard way at J-495). **Known cost:** a custom caption loses Windows Snap-Layouts unless re-implemented — the standard price of custom chrome. **Joe's rule, locked (J-495): no native elements within the window's main pane** — the native chrome is the OS title bar and nothing else, which is why the SE grip stays even while native edges resize.

> **🟡 M-RP6.1e-B1 no-select chrome (PENDING, filed J-495):** **chrome is not text.** Global `user-select: none` in `skin.css` (**L2** — it passes the remove-the-rule litmus: delete the skin and native selection returns, nothing *breaks*), with a deliberate opt-in list. **Selectable:** `input` / `textarea` / `[contenteditable]` — **mandatory, not cosmetic**: an ancestor `none` breaks caret and drag-select inside editable natives in WebView2, so this rule is what keeps `textfield`/`textarea`/`number`/`converter-field`/`combobox`/`tag-select` working — and **`.paragraph`**, the prose primitive and therefore the **message body** (`message` composes `paragraph`; users must be able to copy a message). **Not selectable:** everything else — `label`, `button`, `menu-item`, `chip`, `status-bar`, `led`, `link` text, panel rows, `entity-item` names, `separator`. Extras: `-webkit-user-drag:none` on `img` (kills the ghost-drag on logos/avatars); `::selection` tied to `--accent2` so selection reads as intentional. Touches `skin.css` (core territory, fenced out of 6.1e-B by its D10) → **its own commit**, sampler-verified (computed `user-select` across a spread: `body` none · `.paragraph` text · `input`/`textarea` text · `.label`/`.button` none) + a client eye-check that message-copy still works once the stream lands.

> 🟡 **M-RP-FOCUS — the focus state is its OWN semantic, not the accent. PENDING, skin-only, gates nothing (filed 2026-07-12, Joe-locked).** **The library focuses two different ways today, and one of them is wrong.** **✅ Already fixed (committed, ~Jun 2026):** the **field family** — `textfield` · `textarea` · `number` · `date` · `select` · `select-multiple` · `color` · `converter-field` — indicates focus with a **lighter-grey BORDER-LIFT** (`border-color: var(--t3)` + `outline:none`). *That is the correct treatment: quiet, legible, not shouting.* **❌ Still wrong:** the **action / navigation family** — **~13 components** (`button` · `link` · `toggle` · `range` · `file` · `star-rating` · `file-field` drop-zone · `section-toggle` · `cp-num` · `entity-panel-option` · `entity-context-menu` items · `menu-trigger` · `menu-item`) — still rides `--focus-ring`, a double `box-shadow` glow **hardcoded to `--accent2`** (gold/blue). **An accent is a BRAND colour; focus is a STATE.** Using the brand to say *"you are here"* makes every focus event shout, and it couples an interaction affordance to a per-shell identity knob. **🔑 THE ROOT CAUSE IS A MISSING NAME.** There is **no `--focus` token**: the ring hardcodes `--accent2`, and the (correct) field fix borrows **`--t3` — a TEXT-RAMP token**, so it currently means *"dim text"* rather than *"focused"*. **A token's NAME is what stops the next person re-pointing it at the accent.** **The fix:** introduce **`--focus`** as its own semantic token (light grey; **NOT an alias of `--t3`/`--t4`, NOT the accent**), point **`--focus-ring` at it** — **one line flips all ~13 components off the accent with ZERO component edits** — and give the field family `border-color: var(--focus)`, the same look it has now, correctly named. **Open at kickoff (Joe, HMR-tunable):** does the action family keep the **glow**, or converge on the field family's **border-lift**? *One focus treatment across the whole library is the cleaner answer — the action components have borders too.* **Supersedes “Q5 focus policy: accent” (J-412, the first skin pass)** — a lock, not a drift. **Cost: `ui/assets/skin.css` ONLY.** No component code, no registry change, no Rust — *the N-025/N-090 payoff made literal: appearance is TOTAL in L2, so a library-wide focus policy is one file and one token.* **⚠️ KNOCK-ON, filed so it is never mistaken for a regression:** several shipped milestones assert *“accent-neutral chrome — the focus ring is the ONLY accent carrier”* (menu-bar · status-bar · separator · and M-RP6.1i's DoD leg 7). After this lands that chrome is **FULLY accent-neutral**, and those legs read *“nothing moves under an accent swap.”* **Update the assertion; do not treat the change as a break.** **Slots into any gap between milestones.**

> ⬛ **M-RP-WINSTATE — SUPERSEDED 2026-07-12 (J-510 / D-115). ABSORBED INTO THE UI-STATE STORE AT M-RP6.1k. Not a milestone any more; kept as the record of a question answered by evidence.** **Its own written criterion fired and decided it:** *"At kickoff, did the widget grid produce a persistent UI-state store? **YES → B** · **NO → A**."* **It did → B.** Geometry is **~five keys in the D-114 store**; **`tauri-plugin-window-state` is NOT taken**; one file, one lifecycle. Both mandatory clauses survive and are **binding on 6.1k**: **work-area clamp** (discard + centre if the rect intersects no monitor) and the **unit** — settled as **PHYSICAL px** (**D-115**, N-092b, measured twice). *This entry is the argument for writing a deciding criterion down instead of a preference: the answer cost one grep, and nobody had to re-argue it in a seat that had forgotten the context.* **Original filing, kept as history:** ⏸️ *window size + position memory. POSTPONED, gated on the widget grid (filed J-498, Joe-locked).* The app should remember its geometry across restarts. **Deliberately NOT built now, and deliberately NOT pre-decided.** *Joe's deferral, accepted as the better call:* building geometry persistence **before the widget grid exists** is building against an unknown ("restore the window" means something different once the interior has a saved layout), and **M-RP8** (frameless + custom title-bar) changes how the window is dragged and resized anyway. *Chat's counter-argument, recorded because it does not expire:* geometry is the **first member of a family** — pane splits, the widget-grid layout, theme, last-open panel all need persistence, so a UI-state store **will** exist; taking `tauri-plugin-window-state` today buys a second state file with a second lifecycle. **The A-vs-B case is strongest today and weakest exactly when this arc executes** — so nothing is locked, and the **deciding criterion is written down instead. At kickoff, ask: did the widget grid produce a persistent UI-state store?** → **YES → B** (own it: geometry becomes ~five keys in the existing store; no new dependency, one lifecycle). → **NO → A** (`tauri-plugin-window-state`, justified by the **OS-domain vs app-domain** split — the WM owns the frame, the app owns the interior — **not** by convenience). **Either way, mandatory:** clamp the restored rect to the **monitor work area** (discard + centre if it intersects no monitor — the unplugged-second-screen case), and **settle the logical-vs-physical unit question** (N-092b — the config is applied in **physical** px; measured twice now, J-495 and J-498). **Geometry is ephemeral UI state, NOT user config** — it does **not** belong in `xgen-client_config.toml`, which carries protocol/identity/user intent. **Consequence for now: `1240×1080` is a first-launch default that remembered geometry will override — do not tune it.**
